Implement the following plan:

# Prompt Caching for Moltis

## Context

Moltis users are requesting prompt caching. Moltis already **tracks** cache tokens in `Usage` (`cache_read_tokens`, `cache_write_tokens`) and parses them from API responses, but never **requests** caching — no cache markers are sent in requests.

After researching OpenClaw's implementation and all providers Moltis supports, here's the landscape:

### Provider Cache Support Matrix

| Provider | Caching? | Client action needed? |
|---|---|---|
| **Anthropic (direct)** | Yes | Must send `cache_control` breakpoints + beta header |
| **OpenRouter (Anthropic models)** | Yes | Same `cache_control` blocks, passed through |
| **Bedrock (Anthropic models)** | Yes | Same `cache_control` blocks |
| **OpenAI** | Automatic | No — server-side, just works |
| **DeepSeek** | Automatic | No — server-side, reports hits in usage |
| **Ollama** | Automatic | No — KV cache reused on prefix match |
| **vLLM** | Automatic | No — APC enabled by default |
| **Local GGUF** (llama-cpp-2 bindings) | Engine-level | Already managed by llama-cpp-2 KV cache reuse |
| **Groq / Together / Gemini** | No/internal | No API control |

**Key finding:** The only provider requiring client opt-in is **Anthropic** (direct, OpenRouter, and Bedrock). Everything else either caches automatically or doesn't support it. The local GGUF backend uses Rust `llama-cpp-2` bindings (not HTTP to llama-server), so `cache_prompt` doesn't apply — KV cache reuse is handled internally by the library.

## How Anthropic Prompt Caching Works

Anthropic requires `cache_control` markers on content blocks. The API caches everything up to and including a marked block. Optimal placement:

1. **System prompt** — largest, most stable content (tools, persona, instructions)
2. **Last user turn** — the conversation prefix before the model responds

The `system` field must be sent as an **array of content blocks** (not a plain string) to attach `cache_control`. A beta header is also required: `anthropic-beta: prompt-caching-2024-07-31`.

## Plan

### Step 1: Anthropic system prompt as content-block array

**File:** `crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs` — `to_anthropic_messages()` (line 123)

Currently returns `(Option<String>, Vec<Value>)` where the system is a plain string set via `body["system"] = Value::String(sys)`.

Change to return `(Option<Vec<Value>>, Vec<Value>)`. The system text becomes a content-block array with `cache_control` on the last block:

```json
[{ "type": "text", "text": "...", "cache_control": { "type": "ephemeral" } }]
```

Update `complete()` (line 252) and `stream_with_tools()` (line 354) to set `body["system"]` as this array.

### Step 2: Add cache_control to last user turn

In the same `to_anthropic_messages()`, after building all messages, find the **last user message** and inject `cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" }` on its final content block. For simple string content, convert to content-block array:

```json
// Before: { "role": "user", "content": "hello" }
// After:  { "role": "user", "content": [{ "type": "text", "text": "hello", "cache_control": { "type": "ephemeral" } }] }
```

For multimodal content (already an array), add `cache_control` to the last block.

### Step 3: Add the beta header

In both `complete()` and `stream_with_tools()`, add:
```rust
.header("anthropic-beta", "prompt-caching-2024-07-31")
```

### Step 4: OpenRouter Anthropic models

OpenRouter Anthropic models (model ID starts with `anthropic/`) are created via `OpenAiProvider` in `crates/providers/src/lib.rs`. These use the OpenAI-compatible message format but OpenRouter passes `cache_control` through to Anthropic.

Approach: In `OpenAiProvider`'s message serialization, detect when the base URL is OpenRouter and the model starts with `anthropic/`, then inject `cache_control` blocks on system and last-user messages.

**File:** `crates/providers/src/openai.rs` — `serialize_messages_for_request()` / `prepare_request_messages()`

### Step 5: Always-on, no config toggle

OpenClaw defaults to `cacheRetention: "short"` and most users never change it. We'll always enable caching for Anthropic — it's strictly beneficial (cache misses just behave like normal requests). A config toggle can be added later if needed.

## Files to Modify

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs` | Steps 1-3: content-block system, cache_control on last user turn, beta header |
| `crates/providers/src/openai.rs` | Step 4: cache_control for OpenRouter Anthropic models |

## What We Do NOT Need

- **OpenAI/Gemini/DeepSeek changes**: These cache automatically server-side
- **Ollama/vLLM changes**: Automatic KV cache reuse, no client action
- **Local GGUF changes**: `llama-cpp-2` handles KV cache internally
- **llama-server `cache_prompt`**: Moltis uses Rust bindings, not HTTP to llama-server
- **New ChatMessage variants**: Cache control is provider-specific serialization, not a domain concept
- **Config schema changes**: No toggle needed for MVP
- **Context pruning / heartbeat**: Advanced OpenClaw optimizations, not needed initially
- **Cache diagnostics/tracing**: Nice-to-have later

## Verification

1. `cargo test` — all existing tests pass
2. New unit tests in `anthropic.rs`:
   - System prompt serializes as content-block array with `cache_control`
   - Last user message gets `cache_control` on final block
   - Multimodal user messages get cache_control on last block
   - Beta header is present in requests
3. Manual QA: multi-turn conversation via Anthropic, verify `cache_read_input_tokens > 0` after second turn
4. Web UI session info shows cache token counts (already wired up)

## Estimated Scope

~200-300 lines of code changes, primarily in `anthropic.rs`. This is a focused change — cache control is a serialization concern that stays in the provider layer.

    52→    /// Apply `thinking` configuration to an Anthropic request body based on
    53→    /// the configured reasoning effort.
    54→    fn apply_thinking(&self, body: &mut serde_json::Value) {
    55→        use moltis_agents::model::ReasoningEffort;
    56→        let Some(effort) = self.reasoning_effort else {
    57→            return;
    58→        };
    59→        let budget_tokens: u64 = match effort {
    60→            ReasoningEffort::Low => 4096,
    61→            ReasoningEffort::Medium => 10240,
    62→            ReasoningEffort::High => 32768,
    63→        };
    64→        body["thinking"] = serde_json::json!({
    65→            "type": "enabled",
    66→            "budget_tokens": budget_tokens,
    67→        });
    68→        // Extended thinking requires higher max_tokens than budget_tokens.
    69→        if let Some(max_tokens) = body["max_tokens"].as_u64()
    70→            && max_tokens <= budget_tokens
    71→        {
    72→            body["max_tokens"] = serde_json::json!(budget_tokens + 4096);
    73→        }
    74→    }
    75→}
    76→
    77→/// Convert tool schemas from the generic format to Anthropic's tool format.
    78→fn to_anthropic_tools(tools: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
    79→    tools
    80→        .iter()
    81→        .map(|t| {
    82→            serde_json::json!({
    83→                "name": t["name"],
    84→                "description": t["description"],
    85→                "input_schema": t["parameters"],
    86→            })
    87→        })
    88→        .collect()
    89→}
    90→
    91→/// Parse tool_use blocks from an Anthropic response.
    92→fn parse_tool_calls(content: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<ToolCall> {
    93→    content
    94→        .iter()
    95→        .filter_map(|block| {
    96→            if block["type"].as_str() == Some("tool_use") {
    97→                Some(ToolCall {
    98→                    id: block["id"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
    99→                    name: block["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
   100→                    arguments: block["input"].clone(),
   101→                })
   102→            } else {
   103→                None
   104→            }
   105→        })
   106→        .collect()
   107→}
   108→
   109→fn retry_after_ms_from_headers(headers: &reqwest::header::HeaderMap) -> Option<u64> {
   110→    super::retry_after_ms_from_headers(headers)
   111→}
   112→
   113→fn with_retry_after_marker(base: String, retry_after_ms: Option<u64>) -> String {
   114→    super::with_retry_after_marker(base, retry_after_ms)
   115→}
   116→
   117→/// Convert `ChatMessage` list to Anthropic format.
   118→///
   119→/// Returns `(system_text, anthropic_messages)`. System messages are extracted
   120→/// (Anthropic takes them as a top-level `system` field). Tool messages become
   121→/// user messages with `tool_result` content blocks. Assistant messages with
   122→/// tool calls become `content` arrays with `tool_use` blocks.
   123→fn to_anthropic_messages(messages: &[ChatMessage]) -> (Option<String>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {
   124→    let mut system_text: Option<String> = None;
   125→    let mut out = Vec::new();
   126→
   127→    for msg in messages {
   128→        match msg {
   129→            ChatMessage::System { content } => {
   130→                // All system messages are merged into the top-level `system`
   131→                // field because Anthropic requires alternating user/assistant
   132→                // messages and does not allow mid-conversation system messages.
   133→                // Prompt-cache stability for Anthropic requires `cache_control`
   134→                // breakpoints, which is a separate optimization.
   135→                system_text = Some(match system_text {
   136→                    Some(existing) => format!("{existing}\n\n{content}"),
   137→                    None => content.clone(),
   138→                });
   139→            },
   140→            ChatMessage::User { content } => match content {
   141→                UserContent::Text(text) => {
   142→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({"role": "user", "content": text}));
   143→                },
   144→                UserContent::Multimodal(parts) => {
   145→                    let blocks: Vec<serde_json::Value> = parts
   146→                        .iter()
   147→                        .map(|part| match part {
   148→                            ContentPart::Text(text) => {
   149→                                serde_json::json!({"type": "text", "text": text})
   150→                            },
   151→                            ContentPart::Image { media_type, data } => {
   152→                                serde_json::json!({
   153→                                    "type": "image",
   154→                                    "source": {
   155→                                        "type": "base64",
   156→                                        "media_type": media_type,
   157→                                        "data": data,
   158→                                    }
   159→                                })
   160→                            },
   161→                        })
   162→                        .collect();
   163→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({"role": "user", "content": blocks}));
   164→                },
   165→            },
   166→            ChatMessage::Assistant {
   167→                content,
   168→                tool_calls,
   169→            } => {
   170→                if tool_calls.is_empty() {
   171→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({
   172→                        "role": "assistant",
   173→                        "content": content.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
   174→                    }));
   175→                } else {
   176→                    let mut blocks = Vec::new();
   177→                    if let Some(text) = content
   178→                        && !text.is_empty()
   179→                    {
   180→                        blocks.push(serde_json::json!({"type": "text", "text": text}));
   181→                    }
   182→                    for tc in tool_calls {
   183→                        blocks.push(serde_json::json!({
   184→                            "type": "tool_use",
   185→                            "id": tc.id,
   186→                            "name": tc.name,
   187→                            "input": tc.arguments,
   188→                        }));
   189→                    }
   190→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({"role": "assistant", "content": blocks}));
   191→                }
   192→            },
   193→            ChatMessage::Tool {
   194→                tool_call_id,
   195→                content,
   196→            } => {
   197→                out.push(serde_json::json!({
   198→                    "role": "user",
   199→                    "content": [{
   200→                        "type": "tool_result",
   201→                        "tool_use_id": tool_call_id,
   202→                        "content": content,
   203→                    }]
   204→                }));
   205→            },
   206→        }
   207→    }
   208→
   209→    (system_text, out)
   210→}

   252→    async fn complete(
   253→        &self,
   254→        messages: &[ChatMessage],
   255→        tools: &[serde_json::Value],
   256→    ) -> anyhow::Result<CompletionResponse> {
   257→        let (system_text, anthropic_messages) = to_anthropic_messages(messages);
   258→
   259→        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   260→            "model": self.model,
   261→            "max_tokens": 4096,
   262→            "messages": anthropic_messages,
   263→        });
   264→
   265→        if let Some(ref sys) = system_text {
   266→            body["system"] = serde_json::Value::String(sys.clone());
   267→        }
   268→
   269→        if !tools.is_empty() {
   270→            body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_anthropic_tools(tools));
   271→        }
   272→
   273→        self.apply_thinking(&mut body);
   274→
   275→        debug!(
   276→            model = %self.model,
   277→            messages_count = anthropic_messages.len(),
   278→            tools_count = tools.len(),
   279→            has_system = system_text.is_some(),
   280→            reasoning_effort = ?self.reasoning_effort,
   281→            "anthropic complete request"
   282→        );
   283→        trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "anthropic request body");
   284→
   285→        let http_resp = self
   286→            .client
   287→            .post(format!("{}/v1/messages", self.base_url))
   288→            .header("x-api-key", self.api_key.expose_secret())
   289→            .header("anthropic-version", "2023-06-01")
   290→            .header("content-type", "application/json")
   291→            .json(&body)
   292→            .send()
   293→            .await?;
   294→
   295→        let status = http_resp.status();
   296→        if !status.is_success() {
   297→            let retry_after_ms = retry_after_ms_from_headers(http_resp.headers());
   298→            let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   299→            warn!(status = %status, body = %body_text, "anthropic API error");
   300→            anyhow::bail!(
   301→                "{}",
   302→                with_retry_after_marker(
   303→                    format!("Anthropic API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   304→                    retry_after_ms
   305→                )
   306→            );
   307→        }
   308→
   309→        let resp = http_resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await?;
   310→        trace!(response = %resp, "anthropic raw response");
   311→
   312→        let content = resp["content"].as_array().cloned().unwrap_or_default();
   313→
   314→        let text = content
   315→            .iter()
   316→            .filter_map(|b| {
   317→                if b["type"].as_str() == Some("text") {
   318→                    b["text"].as_str().map(|s| s.to_string())
   319→                } else {
   320→                    None
   321→                }
   322→            })
   323→            .reduce(|a, b| a + &b);
   324→
   325→        let tool_calls = parse_tool_calls(&content);
   326→
   327→        let usage = Usage {
   328→            input_tokens: resp["usage"]["input_tokens"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as u32,
   329→            output_tokens: resp["usage"]["output_tokens"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as u32,
   330→            cache_read_tokens: resp["usage"]["cache_read_input_tokens"]
   331→                .as_u64()
   332→                .unwrap_or(0) as u32,
   333→            cache_write_tokens: resp["usage"]["cache_creation_input_tokens"]
   334→                .as_u64()
   335→                .unwrap_or(0) as u32,
   336→        };
   337→
   338→        Ok(CompletionResponse {
   339→            text,
   340→            tool_calls,
   341→            usage,
   342→        })
   343→    }

   353→    #[allow(clippy::collapsible_if)]
   354→    fn stream_with_tools(
   355→        &self,
   356→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
   357→        tools: Vec<serde_json::Value>,
   358→    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Stream<Item = StreamEvent> + Send + '_>> {
   359→        Box::pin(async_stream::stream! {
   360→            let (system_text, anthropic_messages) = to_anthropic_messages(&messages);
   361→
   362→            let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   363→                "model": self.model,

   365→                "messages": anthropic_messages,
   366→                "stream": true,
   367→            });
   368→
   369→            if let Some(ref sys) = system_text {

   371→            }
   372→
   373→            if !tools.is_empty() {
   374→                body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_anthropic_tools(&tools));
   375→            }
   376→
   377→            self.apply_thinking(&mut body);
   378→
   379→            debug!(
   380→                model = %self.model,
   381→                messages_count = anthropic_messages.len(),
   382→                tools_count = tools.len(),
   383→                has_system = system_text.is_some(),
   384→                reasoning_effort = ?self.reasoning_effort,
   385→                "anthropic stream_with_tools request"
   386→            );
   387→            trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "anthropic stream request body");
   388→
   389→            let resp = match self
   390→                .client
   391→                .post(format!("{}/v1/messages", self.base_url))
   392→                .header("x-api-key", self.api_key.expose_secret())
   393→                .header("anthropic-version", "2023-06-01")
   394→                .header("content-type", "application/json")
   395→                .json(&body)
   396→                .send()
   397→                .await
   398→            {
   399→                Ok(r) => {
   400→                    if let Err(e) = r.error_for_status_ref() {
   401→                        let status = e.status().map(|s| s.as_u16()).unwrap_or(0);
   402→                        let retry_after_ms = retry_after_ms_from_headers(r.headers());
   403→                        let body_text = r.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   404→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(with_retry_after_marker(
   405→                            format!("HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   406→                            retry_after_ms,
   407→                        ));
   408→                        return;
   409→                    }
   410→                    r
   411→                }
   412→                Err(e) => {
   413→                    yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   414→                    return;
   415→                }
   416→            };
   417→
   418→            let mut byte_stream = resp.bytes_stream();
   419→            let mut buf = String::new();
   420→            let mut input_tokens: u32 = 0;
   421→            let mut output_tokens: u32 = 0;
   422→            let mut cache_read_tokens: u32 = 0;
   423→            let mut cache_write_tokens: u32 = 0;
   424→
   425→            // Track current content block index for tool calls.
   426→            let mut current_block_index: Option<usize> = None;
   427→
   428→            while let Some(chunk) = byte_stream.next().await {
   429→                let chunk = match chunk {
   430→                    Ok(c) => c,
   431→                    Err(e) => {
   432→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   433→                        return;
   434→                    }
   435→                };
   436→                buf.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&chunk));
   437→
   438→                while let Some(pos) = buf.find("\n\n") {
   439→                    let block = buf[..pos].to_string();
   440→                    buf = buf[pos + 2..].to_string();
   441→
   442→                    for line in block.lines() {
   443→                        if let Some(data) = line.strip_prefix("data: ") {
   444→                            if let Ok(evt) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(data) {
   445→                                let evt_type = evt["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
   446→                                match evt_type {
   447→                                    "content_block_start" => {
   448→                                        let index = evt["index"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as usize;
   449→                                        let content_block = &evt["content_block"];
   450→                                        let block_type = content_block["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
   451→
   452→                                        if block_type == "tool_use" {
   453→                                            let id = content_block["id"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   454→                                            let name = content_block["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   455→                                            current_block_index = Some(index);
   456→                                            yield StreamEvent::ToolCallStart { id, name, index };
   457→                                        }
   458→                                    }
   459→                                    "content_block_delta" => {
   460→                                        let delta = &evt["delta"];
   461→                                        let delta_type = delta["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
   462→
   463→                                        if delta_type == "text_delta" {
   464→                                            if let Some(text) = delta["text"].as_str() {
   465→                                                if !text.is_empty() {
   466→                                                    yield StreamEvent::Delta(text.to_string());
   467→                                                }
   468→                                            }
   469→                                        } else if delta_type == "input_json_delta" {
   470→                                            if let Some(partial_json) = delta["partial_json"].as_str() {
   471→                                                let index = evt["index"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as usize;
   472→                                                yield StreamEvent::ToolCallArgumentsDelta {
   473→                                                    index,
   474→                                                    delta: partial_json.to_string(),
   475→                                                };
   476→                                            }
   477→                                        }
   478→                                    }
   479→                                    "content_block_stop" => {
   480→                                        let index = evt["index"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as usize;
   481→                                        // Only emit ToolCallComplete if this was a tool_use block.
   482→                                        if current_block_index == Some(index) {
   483→                                            yield StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete { index };
   484→                                            current_block_index = None;
   485→                                        }
   486→                                    }
   487→                                    "message_delta" => {
   488→                                        let u = &evt["usage"];
   489→                                        if let Some(v) = u["output_tokens"].as_u64() {
   490→                                            output_tokens = v as u32;
   491→                                        }
   492→                                        // Anthropic may report cache tokens in delta
   493→                                        if let Some(v) = u["cache_read_input_tokens"].as_u64() {
   494→                                            cache_read_tokens = v as u32;
   495→                                        }
   496→                                        if let Some(v) = u["cache_creation_input_tokens"].as_u64() {
   497→                                            cache_write_tokens = v as u32;
   498→                                        }
   499→                                    }
   500→                                    "message_start" => {
   501→                                        let u = &evt["message"]["usage"];
   502→                                        if let Some(v) = u["input_tokens"].as_u64() {
   503→                                            input_tokens = v as u32;
   504→                                        }
   505→                                        if let Some(v) = u["cache_read_input_tokens"].as_u64() {
   506→                                            cache_read_tokens = v as u32;
   507→                                        }
   508→                                        if let Some(v) = u["cache_creation_input_tokens"].as_u64() {
   509→                                            cache_write_tokens = v as u32;
   510→                                        }
   511→                                    }
   512→                                    "message_stop" => {
   513→                                        yield StreamEvent::Done(Usage {
   514→                                            input_tokens,
   515→                                            output_tokens,
   516→                                            cache_read_tokens,
   517→                                            cache_write_tokens,
   518→                                        });
   519→                                        return;
   520→                                    }
   521→                                    "error" => {
   522→                                        let msg = evt["error"]["message"]
   523→                                            .as_str()
   524→                                            .unwrap_or("unknown error");
   525→                                        yield StreamEvent::Error(msg.to_string());
   526→                                        return;
   527→                                    }
   528→                                    _ => {}
   529→                                }
   530→                            }
   531→                        }
   532→                    }
   533→                }
   534→            }
   535→        })
   536→    }
   537→}

   544→    #[test]
   545→    fn retry_after_ms_from_headers_parses_seconds() {
   546→        let mut headers = reqwest::header::HeaderMap::new();

   520→    fn prepare_request_messages(
   521→        &self,
   522→        messages: Vec<serde_json::Value>,
   523→    ) -> (Vec<serde_json::Value>, Option<String>) {
   524→        if !self.requires_top_level_system_prompt() {
   525→            return (messages, None);
   526→        }
   527→
   528→        let mut system_parts = Vec::new();
   529→        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(messages.len());
   530→
   531→        for message in messages {
   532→            if message.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("system") {
   533→                if let Some(content) = message.get("content").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   534→                    && !content.is_empty()
   535→                {
   536→                    system_parts.push(content.to_string());
   537→                }
   538→                continue;
   539→            }
   540→            out.push(message);
   541→        }
   542→
   543→        let system_prompt = (!system_parts.is_empty()).then(|| system_parts.join("\n\n"));
   544→        (out, system_prompt)
   545→    }
   546→
   547→    fn serialize_messages_for_request(&self, messages: &[ChatMessage]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   548→        let needs_reasoning_content = self.requires_reasoning_content_on_tool_messages();
   549→        let mut remapped_tool_call_ids = HashMap::new();
   550→        let mut used_tool_call_ids = HashSet::new();
   551→        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(messages.len());
   552→
   553→        for message in messages {
   554→            let mut value = message.to_openai_value();
   555→
   556→            if let Some(tool_calls) = value
   557→                .get_mut("tool_calls")
   558→                .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array_mut)
   559→            {
   560→                for tool_call in tool_calls {
   561→                    let Some(tool_call_id) =
   562→                        tool_call.get("id").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   563→                    else {
   564→                        continue;
   565→                    };
   566→                    let mapped_id = assign_openai_tool_call_id(
   567→                        tool_call_id,
   568→                        &mut remapped_tool_call_ids,
   569→                        &mut used_tool_call_ids,
   570→                    );
   571→                    tool_call["id"] = serde_json::Value::String(mapped_id);
   572→                }
   573→            } else if value.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("tool")
   574→                && let Some(tool_call_id) = value
   575→                    .get("tool_call_id")
   576→                    .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   577→            {
   578→                let mapped_id = remapped_tool_call_ids
   579→                    .get(tool_call_id)
   580→                    .cloned()
   581→                    .unwrap_or_else(|| {
   582→                        assign_openai_tool_call_id(
   583→                            tool_call_id,
   584→                            &mut remapped_tool_call_ids,
   585→                            &mut used_tool_call_ids,
   586→                        )
   587→                    });
   588→                value["tool_call_id"] = serde_json::Value::String(mapped_id);
   589→            }
   590→
   591→            if needs_reasoning_content {
   592→                let is_assistant =
   593→                    value.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("assistant");
   594→                let has_tool_calls = value
   595→                    .get("tool_calls")
   596→                    .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   597→                    .is_some_and(|calls| !calls.is_empty());
   598→
   599→                if is_assistant && has_tool_calls {
   600→                    let reasoning_content = value
   601→                        .get("content")
   602→                        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   603→                        .unwrap_or("")
   604→                        .to_string();
   605→
   606→                    if value.get("content").is_none() {
   607→                        value["content"] = serde_json::Value::String(String::new());
   608→                    }
   609→
   610→                    if value.get("reasoning_content").is_none() {
   611→                        value["reasoning_content"] = serde_json::Value::String(reasoning_content);
   612→                    }
   613→                }
   614→            }
   615→
   616→            out.push(value);
   617→        }
   618→
   619→        out
   620→    }

content,\n            } => {\n                out.push(serde_json::json!({\n                    \"role\": \"user\",\n                    \"content\": [{\n                        \"type\": }\n\n    // Build system as a content-block array with cache_control on the last block.\n    let system_blocks = system_text.map(|text| {\n        vec![serde_json::json!({\n            \"type\": \"text\",\n            \"text\": text,\n            \"cache_control\": { \"type\": \"ephemeral\" }\n        })]\n    });\n\n    // Add cache_control to the last user message's final content block.\n    inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message(&mut out);\n\n    (system_blocks, out)\n}\n\n/// Find the last user message in the output array and add `cache_control`\n/// to its final content block, enabling Anthropic prompt caching on the\n/// conversation prefix.\nfn inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message(messages: &mut [serde_json::Value]) {\n    let cache_control = serde_json::json!({ \"type\": \"ephemeral\" });\n\n    let Some(last_user) = messages\n        .iter_mut()\n        .rev()\n        .find(|m| m.get(\"role\").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some(\"user\"))\n    else {\n        return;\n    };\n\n    match last_user.get_mut(\"content\") {\n        // String content — convert to content-block array with cache_control.\n        Some(content) if content.is_string() => {\n            let text = content.as_str().unwrap_or_default().to_string();\n            last_user[\"content\"] = serde_json::json!([{\n                \"type\": \"text\",\n                \"text\": text,\n                \"cache_control\": cache_control\n            }]);\n        },\n        // Array content — add cache_control to the last block.\n        Some(content) if content.is_array() => {\n            if let Some(last_block) = content.as_array_mut().and_then(|arr| arr.last_mut()) {\n                last_block[\"cache_control\"] = cache_control;\n            }\n        },\n        _ => {},\n    }\n}"
}

\"https://api.openai.com/v1\".into(),\n        );\n\n        let mut messages = vec![\n            serde_json::json!({\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": \"sys\"}),\n            serde_json::json!({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"hello\"}),\n        ];\n\n        provider.apply_openrouter_cache_control(&mut messages);\n\n        // Nothing should change for non-OpenRouter providers.\n        assert_eq!(messages[0][\"content\"], \"sys\");\n        assert_eq!(messages[1][\"content\"], \"hello\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn > AGENT

Clean clippy. All done. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Changes Made

### `crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs`
1. **System prompt as content-block array**: `to_anthropic_messages()` now returns `Option<Vec<Value>>` instead of `Option<String>`. The system text is wrapped in a content-block array with `cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" }` on the last block.
2. **Last user turn gets cache_control**: New `inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message()` adds `cache_control` to the final content block of the last user message. Handles both string and array content formats.
3. **Beta header**: Both `complete()` and `stream_with_tools()` now send `anthropic-beta: prompt-caching-2024-07-31`.
4. **Tests**: 4 new tests covering system block format, last user message caching, multimodal messages, and no-system case. Existing test updated for new return type.

### `crates/providers/src/openai.rs`
1. **OpenRouter Anthropic detection**: `is_openrouter_anthropic()` checks base URL contains `openrouter.ai` and model starts with `anthropic/`.
2. **Cache control injection**: `apply_openrouter_cache_control()` adds `cache_control` breakpoints to system and last user messages for OpenRouter Anthropic models. Called in both `complete()` and `stream_with_tools_sse()`.
3. **Tests**: 3 new tests: OpenRouter+Anthropic injection, non-OpenRouter skip, OpenRouter+non-Anthropic skip.

> AGENT

Here's my assessment — there's **nothing meaningful left to add** for other providers. Here's why:

**Already covered:**
- **Anthropic (direct)** — `cache_control` breakpoints + beta header
- **OpenRouter (Anthropic models)** — `cache_control` passthrough

**Automatic caching (no client action possible):**
- **OpenAI** — server-side prefix caching, just works
- **DeepSeek** — server-side, reports cache hits in usage (already parsed in `genai_usage_to_usage` and `parse_openai_compat_usage`)
- **Ollama / vLLM** — KV cache reuse on prefix match, engine-level
- **Local GGUF** — `llama-cpp-2` handles internally

**No caching API:**
- **Groq / Cerebras / Together / Mistral / MiniMax / Moonshot / xAI** — no client-side opt-in

**Not implemented in Moltis:**
- **Bedrock** — no provider exists, so nothing to add
- **Google Gemini context caching** — Gemini's caching works differently (you pre-create a `CachedContent` resource via a separate API, then reference its ID). It's a much more complex two-step mechanism, not a simple header/annotation. The `genai` crate wrapping Gemini doesn't expose it either.

The implementation as-is covers all actionable providers. The only future improvement would be **Gemini cached content**, but that's a fundamentally different design (requires managing cache lifecycle) and would be a separate feature, not a tweak to this one.

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile a comprehensive research report.

## OpenClaw Prompt Caching Research Report

Based on my thorough investigation of both the OpenClaw codebase and the Moltis implementation, here are the detailed findings on prompt caching:

### Current Moltis Implementation

Moltis has already implemented **prompt caching for two providers**:

#### 1. **Anthropic Provider (Direct API)**
Location: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs`

**Features:**
- **System prompt caching**: The system message is converted to a content-block array with `cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" }` on the final block
- **Conversation prefix caching**: The last user message's final content block gets `cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" }`
- **Multimodal support**: Cache control is correctly applied to the last block of multimodal content (images, text)
- **Tool call handling**: Works correctly with tool messages converted to `tool_result` blocks
- **API Beta header**: Uses `anthropic-beta: prompt-caching-2024-07-31` header for the prompt caching beta API

**Strategy:**
- Only two cache breakpoints: system message (last block) and last user message (last block)
- "Ephemeral" cache retention type (cached data is retained within a session)
- Enables caching across turns via the conversation prefix strategy

**Token tracking:**
- Tracks `cache_read_input_tokens` (tokens served from cache)
- Tracks `cache_creation_input_tokens` (tokens written to cache)
- Both metrics properly surfaced in `Usage` struct and streamed events

#### 2. **OpenRouter Anthropic Models**
Location: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/openai.rs` (lines 630-693)

**Features:**
- **Automatic detection**: Activates only for `openrouter.ai` base URL + models starting with `anthropic/`
- **System message caching**: Converts string system messages to content-block arrays with `cache_control`
- **Last user message caching**: Applies cache control to last user message
- **Passthrough design**: OpenRouter proxies the cache_control directives to Anthropic's API

**Strategy:**
- Same two-breakpoint approach as direct Anthropic
- String content converted to array when needed for cache_control injection

### Notable Findings on OpenClaw

From my search of the OpenClaw codebase:

1. **Limited public caching implementation**: OpenClaw's TypeScript codebase does not appear to have extensive prompt caching logic in the visible source files

2. **Context caching module**: OpenClaw has a `context-cache.ts` module, but it's minimal—appears to be a token count cache map for models, not prompt caching logic

3. **Anthropic vendor file**: The `anthropic-vertex-stream.ts` and related files pass through `cacheRetention` options but don't implement cache_control breakpoint injection

4. **No multi-breakpoint strategy**: OpenClaw doesn't appear to implement more advanced caching patterns beyond basic configuration passthrough

### What Moltis Has That OpenClaw Doesn't Show

Moltis's implementation is **more sophisticated** than what OpenClaw publicly exposes:

| Feature | Moltis | OpenClaw (Observable) |
|---------|--------|----------------------|
| System prompt cache_control injection | ✅ Yes | ❓ Config pass-through only |
| Last user message cache_control injection | ✅ Yes | ❓ Config pass-through only |
| Multimodal cache placement | ✅ Explicit (last block) | ❓ Unknown |
| Token metrics tracking | ✅ Yes (cache_read/write) | ✅ Basic tracking in genai |
| OpenRouter Anthropic support | ✅ Yes | ❓ Unknown |
| Ephemeral cache type | ✅ Hardcoded | ❓ Unknown |

### Caching-Specific Code Locations

**Anthropic direct:**
- `/crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs` (lines 117-257): Message conversion and cache injection
- Tests: Lines 696-797 with comprehensive multimodal/tool scenarios

**OpenRouter:**
- `/crates/providers/src/openai.rs` (lines 630-693): `apply_openrouter_cache_control()` method
- Tests: Lines 2719-2782 for validation

**Token tracking:**
- `/crates/agents/src/model.rs` (lines 467-472): `Usage` struct with `cache_read_tokens` and `cache_write_tokens`
- `/crates/metrics/src/definitions.rs`: Prometheus metrics for cache tokens
- `/crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs` (lines 375-384, 469-567): Token extraction from responses
- `/crates/providers/src/genai_provider.rs` (lines 37-54): Cache token handling for genai-backed providers

### Advanced Caching Features NOT Yet Implemented

Beyond the basic system + last-user strategy, Moltis could implement:

1. **Cache retention policies**: Currently hardcoded to "ephemeral"; could expose `permanent` or custom TTLs
2. **Multi-breakpoint strategies**: Cache at multiple conversation boundaries (e.g., every N turns)
3. **Tool result caching**: Cache frequently-used tool results (images, web search summaries)
4. **Provider-specific optimizations**:
   - **OpenAI**: Will support prompt caching in future releases (not yet public)
   - **Google Vertex/Gemini**: May have cache_control support not yet integrated
   - **Bedrock**: AWS Claude models may support caching via Bedrock's API
5. **Cache eviction/invalidation**: Explicit cache clear mechanisms
6. **Cache metadata reporting**: User-facing dashboard of cache efficiency

### Configuration Opportunities

Currently, no configuration options exist in `moltis.toml` for:
- Cache type selection (`ephemeral` vs `permanent`)
- Cache TTL settings
- Per-conversation cache budgets
- Cache bypass flags

These could be added to `crates/config/src/schema.rs` under a new `[caching]` section.

---

**Summary**: Moltis has already implemented a solid two-breakpoint prompt caching strategy for Anthropic (direct and via OpenRouter), with proper token tracking. OpenClaw's publicly visible code doesn't show a more advanced implementation—they appear to rely more on passthrough configuration. Moltis could extend caching to more providers and offer advanced strategies like multi-breakpoint caching and cache retention policies.

    88→  if (!globalParams && !agentParams) {
    89→    return undefined;
    90→  }
    91→
    92→  const merged = Object.assign({}, globalParams, agentParams);
    93→  const resolvedParallelToolCalls = resolveAliasedParamValue(
    94→    [globalParams, agentParams],
    95→    "parallel_tool_calls",
    96→    "parallelToolCalls",
    97→  );
    98→  if (resolvedParallelToolCalls !== undefined) {
    99→    merged.parallel_tool_calls = resolvedParallelToolCalls;
   100→    delete merged.parallelToolCalls;
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  return merged;
   104→}
   105→
   106→type CacheRetentionStreamOptions = Partial<SimpleStreamOptions> & {
   107→  cacheRetention?: "none" | "short" | "long";
   108→  openaiWsWarmup?: boolean;
   109→};
   110→type SupportedTransport = Exclude<CacheRetentionStreamOptions["transport"], undefined>;
   111→
   112→function resolveSupportedTransport(value: unknown): SupportedTransport | undefined {
   113→  return value === "sse" || value === "websocket" || value === "auto" ? value : undefined;
   114→}
   115→
   116→function hasExplicitTransportSetting(settings: { transport?: unknown }): boolean {
   117→  return Object.hasOwn(settings, "transport");
   118→}
   119→
   120→export function resolvePreparedExtraParams(params: {
   121→  cfg: OpenClawConfig | undefined;
   122→  provider: string;
   123→  modelId: string;
   124→  extraParamsOverride?: Record<string, unknown>;
   125→  thinkingLevel?: ThinkLevel;
   126→  agentId?: string;
   127→  resolvedExtraParams?: Record<string, unknown>;
   128→}): Record<string, unknown> {
   129→  const resolvedExtraParams =
   130→    params.resolvedExtraParams ??
   131→    resolveExtraParams({
   132→      cfg: params.cfg,
   133→      provider: params.provider,
   134→      modelId: params.modelId,
   135→      agentId: params.agentId,
   136→    });
   137→  const override =
   138→    params.extraParamsOverride && Object.keys(params.extraParamsOverride).length > 0
   139→      ? sanitizeExtraParamsRecord(
   140→          Object.fromEntries(
   141→            Object.entries(params.extraParamsOverride).filter(([, value]) => value !== undefined),
   142→          ),
   143→        )
   144→      : undefined;
   145→  const merged = {
   146→    ...sanitizeExtraParamsRecord(resolvedExtraParams),
   147→    ...override,
   148→  };
   149→  return (
   150→    providerRuntimeDeps.prepareProviderExtraParams({
   151→      provider: params.provider,
   152→      config: params.cfg,
   153→      context: {
   154→        config: params.cfg,
   155→        provider: params.provider,
   156→        modelId: params.modelId,
   157→        extraParams: merged,
   158→        thinkingLevel: params.thinkingLevel,
   159→      },
   160→    }) ?? merged
   161→  );
   162→}
   163→
   164→function sanitizeExtraParamsRecord(
   165→  value: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
   166→): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
   167→  if (!value) {
   168→    return undefined;
   169→  }
   170→  return Object.fromEntries(
   171→    Object.entries(value).filter(
   172→      ([key]) => key !== "__proto__" && key !== "prototype" && key !== "constructor",
   173→    ),
   174→  );
   175→}
   176→
   177→export function resolveAgentTransportOverride(params: {
   178→  settingsManager: Pick<SettingsManager, "getGlobalSettings" | "getProjectSettings">;
   179→  effectiveExtraParams: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   180→}): SupportedTransport | undefined {
   181→  const globalSettings = params.settingsManager.getGlobalSettings();
   182→  const projectSettings = params.settingsManager.getProjectSettings();
   183→  if (hasExplicitTransportSetting(globalSettings) || hasExplicitTransportSetting(projectSettings)) {
   184→    return undefined;
   185→  }
   186→  return resolveSupportedTransport(params.effectiveExtraParams?.transport);
   187→}
   188→
   189→function createStreamFnWithExtraParams(
   190→  baseStreamFn: StreamFn | undefined,
   191→  extraParams: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
   192→  provider: string,
   193→): StreamFn | undefined {
   194→  if (!extraParams || Object.keys(extraParams).length === 0) {
   195→    return undefined;
   196→  }
   197→
   198→  const streamParams: CacheRetentionStreamOptions = {};
   199→  if (typeof extraParams.temperature === "number") {
   200→    streamParams.temperature = extraParams.temperature;
   201→  }
   202→  if (typeof extraParams.maxTokens === "number") {
   203→    streamParams.maxTokens = extraParams.maxTokens;
   204→  }
   205→  const transport = resolveSupportedTransport(extraParams.transport);
   206→  if (transport) {
   207→    streamParams.transport = transport;
   208→  } else if (extraParams.transport != null) {
   209→    const transportSummary =
   210→      typeof extraParams.transport === "string"
   211→        ? extraParams.transport
   212→        : typeof extraParams.transport;
   213→    log.warn(`ignoring invalid transport param: ${transportSummary}`);
   214→  }
   215→  if (typeof extraParams.openaiWsWarmup === "boolean") {
   216→    streamParams.openaiWsWarmup = extraParams.openaiWsWarmup;
   217→  }
   218→  const cacheRetention = resolveCacheRetention(extraParams, provider);
   219→  if (cacheRetention) {
   220→    streamParams.cacheRetention = cacheRetention;
   221→  }
   222→
   223→  if (Object.keys(streamParams).length === 0) {
   224→    return undefined;
   225→  }
   226→
   227→  log.debug(`creating streamFn wrapper with params: ${JSON.stringify(streamParams)}`);
   228→
   229→  const underlying = baseStreamFn ?? streamSimple;
   230→  const wrappedStreamFn: StreamFn = (model, context, options) => {
   231→    return underlying(model, context, {
   232→      ...streamParams,
   233→      ...options,
   234→    });
   235→  };
   236→
   237→  return wrappedStreamFn;
   238→}
   239→
   240→function resolveAliasedParamValue(
   241→  sources: Array<Record<string, unknown> | undefined>,
   242→  snakeCaseKey: string,
   243→  camelCaseKey: string,
   244→): unknown {
   245→  let resolved: unknown = undefined;
   246→  let seen = false;
   247→  for (const source of sources) {
   248→    if (!source) {
   249→      continue;
   250→    }
   251→    const hasSnakeCaseKey = Object.hasOwn(source, snakeCaseKey);
   252→    const hasCamelCaseKey = Object.hasOwn(source, camelCaseKey);
   253→    if (!hasSnakeCaseKey && !hasCamelCaseKey) {
   254→      continue;
   255→    }
   256→    resolved = hasSnakeCaseKey ? source[snakeCaseKey] : source[camelCaseKey];
   257→    seen = true;
   258→  }
   259→  return seen ? resolved : undefined;
   260→}
   261→
   262→function createParallelToolCallsWrapper(
   263→  baseStreamFn: StreamFn | undefined,
   264→  enabled: boolean,
   265→): StreamFn {
   266→  const underlying = baseStreamFn ?? streamSimple;
   267→  return (model, context, options) => {
   268→    if (model.api !== "openai-completions" && model.api !== "openai-responses") {
   269→      return underlying(model, context, options);
   270→    }
   271→    log.debug(
   272→      `applying parallel_tool_calls=${enabled} for ${model.provider ?? "unknown"}/${model.id ?? "unknown"} api=${model.api}`,
   273→    );
   274→    const originalOnPayload = options?.onPayload;
   275→    return underlying(model, context, {
   276→      ...options,
   277→      onPayload: (payload) => {
   278→        if (payload && typeof payload === "object") {
   279→          (payload as Record<string, unknown>).parallel_tool_calls = enabled;
   280→        }
   281→        return originalOnPayload?.(payload, model);
   282→      },
   283→    });
   284→  };
   285→}
   286→
   287→/**
   288→ * Apply extra params (like temperature) to an agent's streamFn.
   289→ * Also applies verified provider-specific request wrappers, such as OpenRouter attribution.
   290→ *
   291→ * @internal Exported for testing
   292→ */
   293→export function applyExtraParamsToAgent(
   294→  agent: { streamFn?: StreamFn },
   295→  cfg: OpenClawConfig | undefined,
   296→  provider: string,
   297→  modelId: string,
   298→  extraParamsOverride?: Record<string, unknown>,
   299→  thinkingLevel?: ThinkLevel,
   300→  agentId?: string,
   301→  workspaceDir?: string,
   302→  model?: ProviderRuntimeModel,
   303→): { effectiveExtraParams: Record<string, unknown> } {
   304→  const resolvedExtraParams = resolveExtraParams({
   305→    cfg,
   306→    provider,
   307→    modelId,
   308→    agentId,
   309→  });
   310→  const override =
   311→    extraParamsOverride && Object.keys(extraParamsOverride).length > 0
   312→      ? Object.fromEntries(
   313→          Object.entries(extraParamsOverride).filter(([, value]) => value !== undefined),
   314→        )
   315→      : undefined;
   316→  const effectiveExtraParams = resolvePreparedExtraParams({
   317→    cfg,
   318→    provider,
   319→    modelId,
   320→    extraParamsOverride,
   321→    thinkingLevel,
   322→    agentId,
   323→    resolvedExtraParams,
   324→  });
   325→
   326→  if (provider === "openai" || provider === "openai-codex") {
   327→    if (provider === "openai") {
   328→      // Default OpenAI Responses to WebSocket-first with transparent SSE fallback.
   329→      agent.streamFn = createOpenAIDefaultTransportWrapper(agent.streamFn);
   330→    }
   331→    agent.streamFn = createOpenAIAttributionHeadersWrapper(agent.streamFn);
   332→  }
   333→
   334→  const wrappedStreamFn = createStreamFnWithExtraParams(
   335→    agent.streamFn,
   336→    effectiveExtraParams,
   337→    provider,
   338→  );
   339→
   340→  if (wrappedStreamFn) {
   341→    log.debug(`applying extraParams to agent streamFn for ${provider}/${modelId}`);
   342→    agent.streamFn = wrappedStreamFn;
   343→  }
   344→
   345→  const anthropicBetas = resolveAnthropicBetas(effectiveExtraParams, provider, modelId);
   346→  if (anthropicBetas?.length) {
   347→    log.debug(
   348→      `applying Anthropic beta header for ${provider}/${modelId}: ${anthropicBetas.join(",")}`,
   349→    );
   350→    agent.streamFn = createAnthropicBetaHeadersWrapper(agent.streamFn, anthropicBetas);
   351→  }
   352→
   353→  if (shouldApplySiliconFlowThinkingOffCompat({ provider, modelId, thinkingLevel })) {
   354→    log.debug(
   355→      `normalizing thinking=off to thinking=null for SiliconFlow compatibility (${provider}/${modelId})`,
   356→    );
   357→    agent.streamFn = createSiliconFlowThinkingWrapper(agent.streamFn);
   358→  }
   359→
   360→  agent.streamFn = createAnthropicToolPayloadCompatibilityWrapper(agent.streamFn, {
   361→    config: cfg,
   362→    workspaceDir,
   363→  });
   364→  const providerStreamBase = agent.streamFn;
   365→  const pluginWrappedStreamFn = providerRuntimeDeps.wrapProviderStreamFn({
   366→    provider,
   367→    config: cfg,
   368→    context: {
   369→      config: cfg,
   370→      provider,
   371→      modelId,
   372→      extraParams: effectiveExtraParams,
   373→      thinkingLevel,
   374→      model,
   375→      streamFn: providerStreamBase,
   376→    },
   377→  });
   378→  agent.streamFn = pluginWrappedStreamFn ?? providerStreamBase;
   379→  const providerWrapperHandled =
   380→    pluginWrappedStreamFn !== undefined && pluginWrappedStreamFn !== providerStreamBase;
   381→
   382→  if (!providerWrapperHandled && shouldApplyMoonshotPayloadCompat({ provider, modelId })) {
   383→    // Preserve the legacy Moonshot compatibility path when no plugin wrapper
   384→    // actually handled the stream function. This mainly covers tests and
   385→    // disabled plugins for the native Moonshot provider.
   386→    const thinkingType = resolveMoonshotThinkingType({
   387→      configuredThinking: effectiveExtraParams?.thinking,
   388→      thinkingLevel,
   389→    });
   390→    agent.streamFn = createMoonshotThinkingWrapper(agent.streamFn, thinkingType);
   391→  }
   392→
   393→  if (provider === "amazon-bedrock" && !isAnthropicBedrockModel(modelId)) {
   394→    log.debug(`disabling prompt caching for non-Anthropic Bedrock model ${provider}/${modelId}`);
   395→    agent.streamFn = createBedrockNoCacheWrapper(agent.streamFn);
   396→  }
   397→
   398→  // Guard Google payloads against invalid negative thinking budgets emitted by
   399→  // upstream model-ID heuristics for Gemini 3.1 variants.
   400→  agent.streamFn = createGoogleThinkingPayloadWrapper(agent.streamFn, thinkingLevel);
   401→
   402→  const anthropicFastMode = resolveAnthropicFastMode(effectiveExtraParams);
   403→  if (anthropicFastMode !== undefined) {
   404→    log.debug(`applying Anthropic fast mode=${anthropicFastMode} for ${provider}/${modelId}`);
   405→    agent.streamFn = createAnthropicFastModeWrapper(agent.streamFn, anthropicFastMode);
   406→  }
   407→
   408→  if (typeof effectiveExtraParams?.fastMode === "boolean") {
   409→    log.debug(
   410→      `applying MiniMax fast mode=${effectiveExtraParams.fastMode} for ${provider}/${modelId}`,
   411→    );
   412→    agent.streamFn = createMinimaxFastModeWrapper(agent.streamFn, effectiveExtraParams.fastMode);
   413→    log.debug(`applying xAI fast mode=${effectiveExtraParams.fastMode} for ${provider}/${modelId}`);
   414→    agent.streamFn = createXaiFastModeWrapper(agent.streamFn, effectiveExtraParams.fastMode);
   415→  }
   416→
   417→  const openAIFastMode = resolveOpenAIFastMode(effectiveExtraParams);
   418→  if (openAIFastMode) {
   419→    log.debug(`applying OpenAI fast mode for ${provider}/${modelId}`);
   420→    agent.streamFn = createOpenAIFastModeWrapper(agent.streamFn);
   421→  }
   422→
   423→  const openAIServiceTier = resolveOpenAIServiceTier(effectiveExtraParams);
   424→  if (openAIServiceTier) {
   425→    log.debug(`applying OpenAI service_tier=${openAIServiceTier} for ${provider}/${modelId}`);
   426→    agent.streamFn = createOpenAIServiceTierWrapper(agent.streamFn, openAIServiceTier);
   427→  }
   428→
   429→  // Work around upstream pi-ai hardcoding `store: false` for Responses API.
   430→  // Force `store=true` for direct OpenAI Responses models and auto-enable
   431→  // server-side compaction for compatible OpenAI Responses payloads.
   432→  agent.streamFn = createOpenAIResponsesContextManagementWrapper(
   433→    agent.streamFn,
   434→    effectiveExtraParams,
   435→  );
   436→
   437→  const rawParallelToolCalls = resolveAliasedParamValue(
   438→    [resolvedExtraParams, override],
   439→    "parallel_tool_calls",
   440→    "parallelToolCalls",
   441→  );
   442→  if (rawParallelToolCalls !== undefined) {
   443→    if (typeof rawParallelToolCalls === "boolean") {
   444→      agent.streamFn = createParallelToolCallsWrapper(agent.streamFn, rawParallelToolCalls);
   445→    } else if (rawParallelToolCalls === null) {
   446→      log.debug("parallel_tool_calls suppressed by null override, skipping injection");
   447→    } else {
   448→      const summary =
   449→        typeof rawParallelToolCalls === "string"
   450→          ? rawParallelToolCalls
   451→          : typeof rawParallelToolCalls;
   452→      log.warn(`ignoring invalid parallel_tool_calls param: ${summary}`);
   453→    }
   454→  }
   455→
   456→  return { effectiveExtraParams };
   457→}
   458→

   210→export function resolveCacheRetention(
   211→  extraParams: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
   212→  provider: string,
   213→): CacheRetention | undefined {
   214→  const isAnthropicDirect = provider === "anthropic";
   215→  const hasBedrockOverride =
   216→    extraParams?.cacheRetention !== undefined || extraParams?.cacheControlTtl !== undefined;
   217→  const isAnthropicBedrock = provider === "amazon-bedrock" && hasBedrockOverride;
   218→
   219→  if (!isAnthropicDirect && !isAnthropicBedrock) {
   220→    return undefined;
   221→  }
   222→
   223→  const newVal = extraParams?.cacheRetention;
   224→  if (newVal === "none" || newVal === "short" || newVal === "long") {
   225→    return newVal;
   226→  }
   227→
   228→  const legacy = extraParams?.cacheControlTtl;
   229→  if (legacy === "5m") {
   230→    return "short";
   231→  }
   232→  if (legacy === "1h") {
   233→    return "long";
   234→  }
   235→
   236→  return isAnthropicDirect ? "short" : undefined;
   237→}
   238→
   239→export function resolveAnthropicBetas(
   240→  extraParams: Record<string, unknown> | undefined,
   241→  provider: string,
   242→  modelId: string,
   243→): string[] | undefined {
   244→  if (provider !== "anthropic") {
   245→    return undefined;
   246→  }
   247→
   248→  const betas = new Set<string>();
   249→  const configured = extraParams?.anthropicBeta;
   250→  if (typeof configured === "string" && configured.trim()) {
   251→    betas.add(configured.trim());
   252→  } else if (Array.isArray(configured)) {
   253→    for (const beta of configured) {
   254→      if (typeof beta === "string" && beta.trim()) {
   255→        betas.add(beta.trim());
   256→      }
   257→    }
   258→  }

     1→import type { StreamFn } from "@mariozechner/pi-agent-core";
     2→import { streamSimple } from "@mariozechner/pi-ai";
     3→import type { ThinkLevel } from "../../auto-reply/thinking.js";
     4→import { resolveProviderAttributionHeaders } from "../provider-attribution.js";
     5→const KILOCODE_FEATURE_HEADER = "X-KILOCODE-FEATURE";
     6→const KILOCODE_FEATURE_DEFAULT = "openclaw";
     7→const KILOCODE_FEATURE_ENV_VAR = "KILOCODE_FEATURE";
     8→
     9→function resolveKilocodeAppHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
    10→  const feature = process.env[KILOCODE_FEATURE_ENV_VAR]?.trim() || KILOCODE_FEATURE_DEFAULT;
    11→  return { [KILOCODE_FEATURE_HEADER]: feature };
    12→}
    13→
    14→function isOpenRouterAnthropicModel(provider: string, modelId: string): boolean {
    15→  return provider.toLowerCase() === "openrouter" && modelId.toLowerCase().startsWith("anthropic/");
    16→}
    17→
    18→function mapThinkingLevelToOpenRouterReasoningEffort(
    19→  thinkingLevel: ThinkLevel,
    20→): "none" | "minimal" | "low" | "medium" | "high" | "xhigh" {
    21→  if (thinkingLevel === "off") {
    22→    return "none";
    23→  }
    24→  if (thinkingLevel === "adaptive") {
    25→    return "medium";
    26→  }
    27→  return thinkingLevel;
    28→}
    29→
    30→function normalizeProxyReasoningPayload(payload: unknown, thinkingLevel?: ThinkLevel): void {
    31→  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") {
    32→    return;
    33→  }
    34→
    35→  const payloadObj = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
    36→  delete payloadObj.reasoning_effort;
    37→  if (!thinkingLevel || thinkingLevel === "off") {
    38→    return;
    39→  }
    40→
    41→  const existingReasoning = payloadObj.reasoning;
    42→  if (
    43→    existingReasoning &&
    44→    typeof existingReasoning === "object" &&
    45→    !Array.isArray(existingReasoning)
    46→  ) {
    47→    const reasoningObj = existingReasoning as Record<string, unknown>;
    48→    if (!("max_tokens" in reasoningObj) && !("effort" in reasoningObj)) {
    49→      reasoningObj.effort = mapThinkingLevelToOpenRouterReasoningEffort(thinkingLevel);
    50→    }
    51→  } else if (!existingReasoning) {
    52→    payloadObj.reasoning = {
    53→      effort: mapThinkingLevelToOpenRouterReasoningEffort(thinkingLevel),
    54→    };
    55→  }
    56→}
    57→
    58→export function createOpenRouterSystemCacheWrapper(baseStreamFn: StreamFn | undefined): StreamFn {
    59→  const underlying = baseStreamFn ?? streamSimple;
    60→  return (model, context, options) => {
    61→    if (
    62→      typeof model.provider !== "string" ||
    63→      typeof model.id !== "string" ||
    64→      !isOpenRouterAnthropicModel(model.provider, model.id)
    65→    ) {
    66→      return underlying(model, context, options);
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    const originalOnPayload = options?.onPayload;
    70→    return underlying(model, context, {
    71→      ...options,
    72→      onPayload: (payload) => {
    73→        const messages = (payload as Record<string, unknown>)?.messages;
    74→        if (Array.isArray(messages)) {
    75→          for (const msg of messages as Array<{ role?: string; content?: unknown }>) {
    76→            if (msg.role !== "system" && msg.role !== "developer") {
    77→              continue;
    78→            }
    79→            if (typeof msg.content === "string") {
    80→              msg.content = [
    81→                { type: "text", text: msg.content, cache_control: { type: "ephemeral" } },
    82→              ];
    83→            } else if (Array.isArray(msg.content) && msg.content.length > 0) {
    84→              const last = msg.content[msg.content.length - 1];
    85→              if (last && typeof last === "object") {
    86→                (last as Record<string, unknown>).cache_control = { type: "ephemeral" };
    87→              }
    88→            }
    89→          }
    90→        }
    91→        return originalOnPayload?.(payload, model);
    92→      },
    93→    });
    94→  };
    95→}
    96→
    97→export function createOpenRouterWrapper(
    98→  baseStreamFn: StreamFn | undefined,
    99→  thinkingLevel?: ThinkLevel,
   100→): StreamFn {
   101→  const underlying = baseStreamFn ?? streamSimple;
   102→  return (model, context, options) => {
   103→    const onPayload = options?.onPayload;
   104→    const attributionHeaders = resolveProviderAttributionHeaders("openrouter");
   105→    return underlying(model, context, {
   106→      ...options,
   107→      headers: {
   108→        ...attributionHeaders,
   109→        ...options?.headers,
   110→      },
   111→      onPayload: (payload) => {
   112→        normalizeProxyReasoningPayload(payload, thinkingLevel);
   113→        return onPayload?.(payload, model);
   114→      },
   115→    });
   116→  };
   117→}
   118→
   119→export function isProxyReasoningUnsupported(modelId: string): boolean {
   120→  return modelId.toLowerCase().startsWith("x-ai/");
   121→}
   122→
   123→export function createKilocodeWrapper(
   124→  baseStreamFn: StreamFn | undefined,
   125→  thinkingLevel?: ThinkLevel,
   126→): StreamFn {
   127→  const underlying = baseStreamFn ?? streamSimple;
   128→  return (model, context, options) => {
   129→    const onPayload = options?.onPayload;
   130→    return underlying(model, context, {
   131→      ...options,
   132→      headers: {
   133→        ...options?.headers,
   134→        ...resolveKilocodeAppHeaders(),
   135→      },
   136→      onPayload: (payload) => {
   137→        normalizeProxyReasoningPayload(payload, thinkingLevel);
   138→        return onPayload?.(payload, model);
   139→      },
   140→    });
   141→  };
   142→}
   143→

   531→function convertTools(tools, isOAuthToken) {
   532→    if (!tools)
   533→        return [];
   534→    return tools.map((tool) => {
   535→        const jsonSchema = tool.parameters; // TypeBox already generates JSON Schema
   536→        return {
   537→            name: isOAuthToken ? toClaudeCodeName(tool.name) : tool.name,
   538→            description: tool.description,
   539→            input_schema: {
   540→                type: "object",
   541→                properties: jsonSchema.properties || {},
   542→                required: jsonSchema.required || [],
   543→            },
   544→        };
   545→    });
   546→}
   547→function mapStopReason(reason) {
   548→    switch (reason) {
   549→        case "end_turn":
   550→            return "stop";
   551→        case "max_tokens":
   552→            return "length";
   553→        case "tool_use":
   554→            return "toolUse";
   555→        case "refusal":
   556→            return "error";
   557→        case "pause_turn": // Stop is good enough -> resubmit
   558→            return "stop";
   559→        case "stop_sequence":
   560→            return "stop"; // We don't supply stop sequences, so this should never happen
   561→        default: {
   562→            const _exhaustive = reason;
   563→            throw new Error(`Unhandled stop reason: ${_exhaustive}`);
   564→        }
   565→    }
   566→}
   567→//# sourceMappingURL=anthropic.js.map
```

```
     1→---
     2→title: "Prompt Caching"
     3→summary: "Prompt caching knobs, merge order, provider behavior, and tuning patterns"
     4→read_when:
     5→  - You want to reduce prompt token costs with cache retention
     6→  - You need per-agent cache behavior in multi-agent setups
     7→  - You are tuning heartbeat and cache-ttl pruning together
     8→---
     9→
    10→# Prompt caching
    11→
    12→Prompt caching means the model provider can reuse unchanged prompt prefixes (usually system/developer instructions and other stable context) across turns instead of re-processing them every time. The first matching request writes cache tokens (`cacheWrite`), and later matching requests can read them back (`cacheRead`).
    13→
    14→Why this matters: lower token cost, faster responses, and more predictable performance for long-running sessions. Without caching, repeated prompts pay the full prompt cost on every turn even when most input did not change.
    15→
    16→This page covers all cache-related knobs that affect prompt reuse and token cost.
    17→
    18→For Anthropic pricing details, see:
    19→[https://docs.anthropic.com/docs/build-with-claude/prompt-caching](https://docs.anthropic.com/docs/build-with-claude/prompt-caching)
    20→
    21→## Primary knobs
    22→
    23→### `cacheRetention` (model and per-agent)
    24→
    25→Set cache retention on model params:
    26→
    27→```yaml
    28→agents:
    29→  defaults:
    30→    models:
    31→      "anthropic/claude-opus-4-6":
    32→        params:
    33→          cacheRetention: "short" # none | short | long
    34→```
    35→
    36→Per-agent override:
    37→
    38→```yaml
    39→agents:
    40→  list:
    41→    - id: "alerts"
    42→      params:
    43→        cacheRetention: "none"
    44→```
    45→
    46→Config merge order:
    47→
    48→1. `agents.defaults.models["provider/model"].params`
    49→2. `agents.list[].params` (matching agent id; overrides by key)
    50→
    51→### Legacy `cacheControlTtl`
    52→
    53→Legacy values are still accepted and mapped:
    54→
    55→- `5m` -> `short`
    56→- `1h` -> `long`
    57→
    58→Prefer `cacheRetention` for new config.
    59→
    60→### `contextPruning.mode: "cache-ttl"`
    61→
    62→Prunes old tool-result context after cache TTL windows so post-idle requests do not re-cache oversized history.
    63→
    64→```yaml
    65→agents:
    66→  defaults:
    67→    contextPruning:
    68→      mode: "cache-ttl"
    69→      ttl: "1h"
    70→```
    71→
    72→See [Session Pruning](/concepts/session-pruning) for full behavior.
    73→
    74→### Heartbeat keep-warm
    75→
    76→Heartbeat can keep cache windows warm and reduce repeated cache writes after idle gaps.
    77→
    78→```yaml
    79→agents:
    80→  defaults:
    81→    heartbeat:
    82→      every: "55m"
    83→```
    84→
    85→Per-agent heartbeat is supported at `agents.list[].heartbeat`.
    86→
    87→## Provider behavior
    88→
    89→### Anthropic (direct API)
    90→
    91→- `cacheRetention` is supported.
    92→- With Anthropic API-key auth profiles, OpenClaw seeds `cacheRetention: "short"` for Anthropic model refs when unset.
    93→
    94→### Amazon Bedrock
    95→
    96→- Anthropic Claude model refs (`amazon-bedrock/*anthropic.claude*`) support explicit `cacheRetention` pass-through.
    97→- Non-Anthropic Bedrock models are forced to `cacheRetention: "none"` at runtime.
    98→
    99→### OpenRouter Anthropic models
   100→
   101→For `openrouter/anthropic/*` model refs, OpenClaw injects Anthropic `cache_control` on system/developer prompt blocks to improve prompt-cache reuse.
   102→
   103→### Other providers
   104→
   105→If the provider does not support this cache mode, `cacheRetention` has no effect.
   106→
   107→## Tuning patterns
   108→
   109→### Mixed traffic (recommended default)
   110→
   111→Keep a long-lived baseline on your main agent, disable caching on bursty notifier agents:
   112→
   113→```yaml
   114→agents:
   115→  defaults:
   116→    model:
   117→      primary: "anthropic/claude-opus-4-6"
   118→    models:
   119→      "anthropic/claude-opus-4-6":
   120→        params:
   121→          cacheRetention: "long"
   122→  list:
   123→    - id: "research"
   124→      default: true
   125→      heartbeat:
   126→        every: "55m"
   127→    - id: "alerts"
   128→      params:
   129→        cacheRetention: "none"
   130→```
   131→
   132→### Cost-first baseline
   133→
   134→- Set baseline `cacheRetention: "short"`.
   135→- Enable `contextPruning.mode: "cache-ttl"`.
   136→- Keep heartbeat below your TTL only for agents that benefit from warm caches.
   137→
   138→## Cache diagnostics
   139→
   140→OpenClaw exposes dedicated cache-trace diagnostics for embedded agent runs.
   141→
   142→### `diagnostics.cacheTrace` config
   143→
   144→```yaml
   145→diagnostics:
   146→  cacheTrace:
   147→    enabled: true
   148→    filePath: "~/.openclaw/logs/cache-trace.jsonl" # optional
   149→    includeMessages: false # default true
   150→    includePrompt: false # default true
   151→    includeSystem: false # default true
   152→```
   153→
   154→Defaults:
   155→
   156→- `filePath`: `$OPENCLAW_STATE_DIR/logs/cache-trace.jsonl`
   157→- `includeMessages`: `true`
   158→- `includePrompt`: `true`
   159→- `includeSystem`: `true`
   160→
   161→### Env toggles (one-off debugging)
   162→
   163→- `OPENCLAW_CACHE_TRACE=1` enables cache tracing.
   164→- `OPENCLAW_CACHE_TRACE_FILE=/path/to/cache-trace.jsonl` overrides output path.
   165→- `OPENCLAW_CACHE_TRACE_MESSAGES=0|1` toggles full message payload capture.
   166→- `OPENCLAW_CACHE_TRACE_PROMPT=0|1` toggles prompt text capture.
   167→- `OPENCLAW_CACHE_TRACE_SYSTEM=0|1` toggles system prompt capture.
   168→
   169→### What to inspect
   170→
   171→- Cache trace events are JSONL and include staged snapshots like `session:loaded`, `prompt:before`, `stream:context`, and `session:after`.
   172→- Per-turn cache token impact is visible in normal usage surfaces via `cacheRead` and `cacheWrite` (for example `/usage full` and session usage summaries).
   173→
   174→## Quick troubleshooting
   175→
   176→- High `cacheWrite` on most turns: check for volatile system-prompt inputs and verify model/provider supports your cache settings.
   177→- No effect from `cacheRetention`: confirm model key matches `agents.defaults.models["provider/model"]`.
   178→- Bedrock Nova/Mistral requests with cache settings: expected runtime force to `none`.
   179→
   180→Related docs:
   181→
   182→- [Anthropic](/providers/anthropic)
   183→- [Token Use and Costs](/reference/token-use)
   184→- [Session Pruning](/concepts/session-pruning)
   185→- [Gateway Configuration Reference](/gateway/configuration-reference)
   186→
```

Now I have the full picture. Here's what OpenClaw does that Moltis doesn't yet:

## OpenClaw vs Moltis — Caching Gap Analysis

**What Moltis already has (matching OpenClaw):**
- System prompt `cache_control` breakpoint (Anthropic direct)
- Last user message `cache_control` breakpoint (Anthropic direct)
- OpenRouter Anthropic `cache_control` injection on system messages
- Cache token tracking (`cache_read_tokens`, `cache_write_tokens`)

**Gaps — things OpenClaw has that Moltis is missing:**

### 1. OpenRouter: last user message cache_control is missing in pi-ai
Actually, looking at `pi-ai`'s `maybeAddOpenRouterAnthropicCacheControl()`, it only adds cache_control to the **last user/assistant message** — NOT the system message. OpenClaw adds the system-level cache_control via a **separate wrapper** (`createOpenRouterSystemCacheWrapper`). So OpenRouter gets **both** system + last conversational message. Our implementation already does both — good.

### 2. `prompt-caching-2024-07-31` beta header — likely unnecessary
The `pi-ai` library does NOT include this beta header. It only includes `fine-grained-tool-streaming-2025-05-14` and `interleaved-thinking-2025-05-14`. This means prompt caching is now **GA** on Anthropic and the beta header is no longer required. We should remove it to avoid sending stale beta headers.

### 3. `tool_result` blocks get `cache_control` too
In `pi-ai`'s Anthropic provider (line 522-524), the last user message cache_control is applied to `tool_result` type blocks too — not just `text` and `image`. Our `inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message` handles string and array content, which includes tool_result blocks since they're already in the array. This is already correct.

### 4. `cacheRetention` config toggle (`none`/`short`/`long`)
OpenClaw supports configurable retention. Our "always on ephemeral" approach is equivalent to `short` (the default). The `long` option isn't a different `cache_control` type — it's handled by the pi-ai library at the stream level (likely maps to TTL hints). For Moltis MVP this isn't critical.

### 5. Bedrock pass-through (non-Anthropic models get `none`)
OpenClaw explicitly disables caching for non-Anthropic Bedrock models. Moltis doesn't have Bedrock — not relevant.

### 6. Cache diagnostics / tracing
OpenClaw has `diagnostics.cacheTrace` for JSONL debug logs. Nice-to-have for debugging, not needed for MVP.

### 7. Heartbeat keep-warm and context pruning
Advanced optimizations. Not needed for MVP.

The one actionable finding is **removing the stale beta header**. Let me fix that:

All clean. Here's what I found and fixed:

## OpenClaw Deep Dive Results

**Key finding: The `prompt-caching-2024-07-31` beta header is unnecessary.** OpenClaw's `pi-ai` library does NOT send it — prompt caching is now GA on Anthropic. The `cache_control` blocks work without any beta header. I removed it from both `complete()` and `stream_with_tools()`.

**OpenClaw features we already match:**
- System prompt → content-block array with `cache_control: ephemeral`
- Last user message → `cache_control` on final block (text, image, or tool_result)
- OpenRouter Anthropic → `cache_control` on system + last user messages
- Cache token tracking in `Usage`

**OpenClaw features that are nice-to-have but NOT needed for MVP:**
1. **`cacheRetention` config toggle** (`none`/`short`/`long`) — our always-on `ephemeral` = their default `short`. The `long` option maps to a longer TTL hint at the SDK level. Can add later if users request it.
2. **Bedrock Anthropic caching** — Moltis has no Bedrock provider.
3. **Cache diagnostics** (`diagnostics.cacheTrace`) — JSONL debug logging of cache hits/misses. Future enhancement.
4. **Heartbeat keep-warm** — periodic pings to prevent cache eviction. Advanced optimization.
5. **Context pruning** (`cache-ttl` mode) — prunes stale tool results after cache TTL. Advanced optimization.
