## Comprehensive Configuration and Provider Flow Analysis - Moltis Rust Codebase

Based on my thorough exploration of the Moltis codebase, here is the complete configuration and provider flow architecture:

---

## 1. CONFIGURATION LAYER

### 1.1 MoltisConfig Structure
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/schema.rs` (Lines 203-232)

```
MoltisConfig (root config struct)
├── server: ServerConfig
├── providers: ProvidersConfig
├── chat: ChatConfig
├── tools: ToolsConfig
├── agents: AgentsConfig
├── skills: SkillsConfig
├── mcp: McpConfig
├── channels: ChannelsConfig
├── tls: TlsConfig
├── auth: AuthConfig
├── graphql: GraphqlConfig
├── metrics: MetricsConfig
├── identity: AgentIdentity
├── user: UserProfile
├── hooks: Option<HooksConfig>
├── memory: MemoryEmbeddingConfig
├── tailscale: TailscaleConfig
├── failover: FailoverConfig
├── heartbeat: HeartbeatConfig
├── voice: VoiceConfig
├── cron: CronConfig
├── caldav: CalDavConfig
├── webhooks: WebhooksConfig
└── env: HashMap<String, String>
```

### 1.2 ProvidersConfig Structure
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/schema.rs` (Lines 2154-2169)

```rust
pub struct ProvidersConfig {
    /// Optional allowlist of enabled providers (also controls UI picker visibility)
    pub offered: Vec<String>,
    
    /// Provider-specific settings keyed by provider name
    /// Known keys: "anthropic", "openai", "gemini", "groq", "xai", "deepseek", etc.
    #[serde(flatten)]
    pub providers: HashMap<String, ProviderEntry>,
    
    /// Local model IDs from local-llm.json (populated at runtime, not persisted)
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub local_models: Vec<String>,
}
```

### 1.3 ProviderEntry Structure
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/schema.rs` (Lines 2218-2274)

```rust
pub struct ProviderEntry {
    /// Whether this provider is enabled (defaults to true)
    pub enabled: bool,

    /// Override the API key (optional; env var still takes precedence if set)
    pub api_key: Option<Secret<String>>,

    /// Override the base URL (accepts legacy `url` as alias)
    pub base_url: Option<String>,

    /// Preferred model IDs for this provider (shown first in model pickers)
    pub models: Vec<String>,

    /// Whether to fetch provider model catalogs dynamically
    pub fetch_models: bool,  // defaults to true

    /// Streaming transport: "sse", "websocket", or "auto"
    pub stream_transport: ProviderStreamTransport,  // defaults to Sse

    /// Wire format: "chat-completions" or "responses" (OpenAI Responses API)
    pub wire_api: WireApi,  // defaults to ChatCompletions

    /// Optional alias for this provider instance (used in metrics labels)
    pub alias: Option<String>,

    /// How tool calling is handled: "auto", "native", "text", or "off"
    pub tool_mode: ToolMode,  // defaults to Auto
}
```

**Important:** There are NO per-model or per-agent `params` or `extra_params` fields in the config. Provider configuration is flat and applies to all models under that provider.

### 1.4 Template Location
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/template.rs`

This file generates the default TOML template shown to users on first run. It contains detailed comments explaining all 700+ config lines. Key provider config example:

```toml
[providers]
offered = ["local-llm", "github-copilot", "openai-codex", "openai", "anthropic", ...]

[providers.anthropic]
enabled = true
api_key = "sk-ant-..."  # Or set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY env var
models = ["claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929"]  # Optional preferred models
fetch_models = true
base_url = "https://api.anthropic.com"
alias = "anthropic"

[providers.openai]
models = ["gpt-5.3", "gpt-5.2"]
stream_transport = "sse"
```

### 2.1 Schema Mapping
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/validate.rs` (Lines 108-124)

```rust
fn build_schema_map() -> KnownKeys {
    let provider_entry = || {
        Struct(HashMap::from([
            ("enabled", Leaf),
            ("api_key", Leaf),
            ("base_url", Leaf),
            ("url", Leaf),  // legacy alias
            ("models", Leaf),
            ("fetch_models", Leaf),
            ("stream_transport", Leaf),
            ("wire_api", Leaf),
            ("alias", Leaf),
            ("tool_mode", Leaf),
        ]))
    };
    // ... full schema for entire config
}
```

### 2.2 Semantic Warnings
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/validate.rs` (Lines 1011-1300)

The `check_semantic_warnings()` function validates:
- Auth disabled on non-localhost binds
- TLS disabled on non-localhost binds
- Unknown provider names (at line ~1000)
- Unknown channel types
- Unknown tool_mode values (see line 1138-1141 comments)
- Unknown sandbox backends
- Invalid SSRF allowlist CIDR ranges
- And 30+ more semantic checks

**Provider validation:** Checks if provider names in config are known (against `KNOWN_PROVIDERS` constant), warns if unknown.

## 3. PROVIDER INSTANTIATION LAYER

### 3.1 ProviderRegistry Initialization
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/lib.rs` (Lines 1342-1425)

Flow:

```
ProviderRegistry::from_env_with_config_and_overrides(config, env_overrides)
    ↓
    1. fire_discoveries(config, env_overrides)  [parallel HTTP requests]
    2. collect_discoveries(pending)  [blocking wait]
    3. from_config_with_prefetched(config, env_overrides, prefetched)
        ├── register_builtin_providers(config, env_overrides, prefetched)
        │   ├── Anthropic (lines 1970-2016)
        │   └── OpenAI (lines 2019-2077)
        ├── register_openai_compatible_providers(config, ...)  [Gemini, Groq, etc.]
        ├── register_custom_providers(config, ...)  [custom-*]
        ├── register_async_openai_providers(config, ...)  [if feature enabled]
        ├── register_genai_providers(config, ...)  [if feature enabled]
        ├── register_openai_codex_providers(config, ...)  [if feature enabled]
        ├── register_github_copilot_providers(config, ...)  [if feature enabled]
        ├── register_kimi_code_providers(config, ...)  [if feature enabled]
        └── register_local_gguf_providers(config)  [if feature enabled]
```

### 3.2 AnthropicProvider Creation
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/lib.rs` (Lines 2000-2014)

```rust
// For each Anthropic model:
let provider = Arc::new(anthropic::AnthropicProvider::with_alias(
    key.clone(),           // API key from config or env
    model_id.clone(),      // e.g., "claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929"
    base_url.clone(),      // from config or env, defaults to "https://api.anthropic.com"
    alias.clone(),         // from config (optional), for metrics labels
));
```

**AnthropicProvider struct definition:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs` (Lines 12-21)

```rust
pub struct AnthropicProvider {
    api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
    model: String,
    base_url: String,
    client: &'static reqwest::Client,  // shared HTTP client
    alias: Option<String>,  // for metrics
    reasoning_effort: Option<moltis_agents::model::ReasoningEffort>,
}
```

Constructor: `AnthropicProvider::with_alias(api_key, model, base_url, alias)` (lines 36-50)

### 3.3 OpenAiProvider Creation
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/lib.rs` (Lines 2058-2065)

```rust
let provider = Arc::new(
    openai::OpenAiProvider::new_with_name(
        key.clone(),
        model_id.clone(),
        base_url.clone(),
        provider_label.clone(),
    )
    .with_stream_transport(stream_transport)  // from config
);
```

**OpenAiProvider struct definition:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/openai.rs` (Lines 32-44)

```rust
pub struct OpenAiProvider {
    api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
    model: String,
    base_url: String,
    provider_name: String,  // from config or default
    client: &'static reqwest::Client,
    stream_transport: ProviderStreamTransport,  // from config
    wire_api: WireApi,  // from config
    metadata_cache: tokio::sync::OnceCell<ModelMetadata>,
    tool_mode_override: Option<moltis_config::ToolMode>,  // from config
    reasoning_effort: Option<moltis_agents::model::ReasoningEffort>,
}
```

Constructor chain:
- `OpenAiProvider::new_with_name(api_key, model, base_url, provider_label)`
- `.with_stream_transport(stream_transport)` — builder method
- `.with_tool_mode(tool_mode)` — builder method (optional, from config)
- `.with_wire_api(wire_api)` — builder method (optional, from config)

### 3.4 Model Discovery & Registration
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/lib.rs` (Lines 1433-1610)

For each enabled provider:

1. **Check enabled:** `config.is_enabled("openai")`
2. **Resolve API key:** `resolve_api_key(config, "openai", "OPENAI_API_KEY", env_overrides)`
3. **Get base URL:** From config, env, or default
4. **Check fetch_models:** `should_fetch_models(config, "openai")` (controls whether to make HTTP `/v1/models` call)
5. **Get preferred models:** `configured_models_for_provider(config, "openai")` (from config `models` field)
6. **Fire discovery:** Parallel HTTP requests to `/v1/models` endpoints (blocking wait in phase 2)
7. **Merge:** Preferred models shown first, then discovered models
8. **Register:** For each model, create provider instance and register in ProviderRegistry

**Key functions:**
- `resolve_api_key()` — checks config entry, env var, then env_overrides
- `configured_models_for_provider(config, provider_name)` — returns config `models` field
- `should_fetch_models(config, provider)` — checks config entry `.fetch_models` (defaults to true)
- `merge_preferred_and_discovered_models(preferred, discovered)` — prefers config pins

---

## 4. GATEWAY USAGE LAYER

### 4.1 Provider Registry in Gateway State
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/gateway/src/state.rs`

The ProviderRegistry is stored in the gateway state and used when the user selects a model or an agent run needs to execute.

### 4.2 Session Message Storage
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/gateway/src/session.rs`

Messages are persisted as **`Vec<serde_json::Value>`** in the database, with metadata:

```rust
// At gateway boundary (chat.rs)
let messages: Vec<Value> = session.messages.clone();
let typed_messages: Vec<ChatMessage> = values_to_chat_messages(&messages);

// At LLM call:
let response = provider.complete(&typed_messages, tools, ...).await;

// Back to JSON for storage:
let value = serde_json::to_value(&response)?;
session.messages.push(value);  // persisted to SQLite
```

Each message is stored with metadata:
- `created_at`: timestamp
- `model`: which model was used
- `provider`: which provider was used
- `inputTokens`, `outputTokens`: from response usage
- Plus full message content as JSON

### 4.3 Provider Selection Flow
User → Web UI → Gateway RPC → Chat service → ProviderRegistry::get(model_id)

1. User selects model (or uses `/model <name>`)
2. Gateway resolves model ID from ProviderRegistry
3. Looks up LlmProvider trait impl for that model
4. Calls `provider.complete()` or `provider.stream()`
5. Provider makes HTTP request to API (Anthropic, OpenAI, etc.)
6. Response is streamed or buffered back to client
7. Session is persisted with message + usage metadata

---

## 5. REASONING EFFORT (Extended Thinking)

### 5.1 Configuration
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/schema.rs` (Lines 10-24)

```rust
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum ReasoningEffort {
    Low,
    Medium,
    High,
}

// In AgentPreset (line 357-362):
pub reasoning_effort: Option<ReasoningEffort>,
```

Maps to:
- **Anthropic:** `thinking.budget_tokens` (low=4096, medium=10240, high=32768)
- **OpenAI o-series:** `reasoning_effort` field on completion API

### 5.2 Provider Implementation
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs` (Lines 52-74)

```rust
fn apply_thinking(&self, body: &mut serde_json::Value) {
    let Some(effort) = self.reasoning_effort else {
        return;
    };
    let budget_tokens: u64 = match effort {
        ReasoningEffort::Low => 4096,
        ReasoningEffort::Medium => 10240,
        ReasoningEffort::High => 32768,
    };
    body["thinking"] = serde_json::json!({
        "type": "enabled",
        "budget_tokens": budget_tokens,
    });
}
```

## 6. TRACING & METRICS INFRASTRUCTURE

### 6.1 Metrics System
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/docs/src/metrics-and-tracing.md`

Metrics are feature-gated via `metrics` and `prometheus` features:

**Built-in metrics:**
- `moltis_llm_completions_total` (counter) — labeled by `provider`, `model`
- `moltis_llm_input_tokens_total` — labeled by `provider`, `model`
- `moltis_llm_output_tokens_total` — labeled by `provider`, `model`
- `moltis_llm_completion_duration_seconds` (histogram)
- `moltis_http_requests_total`, `moltis_tool_executions_total`, etc.

**Provider aliases:** When `alias` is set on a provider entry, it appears in the `provider` label instead of the provider name (for billing/cost tracking).

Exposed via:
- `/metrics` — Prometheus text format (unauthenticated)
- `/api/metrics` — JSON (authenticated)
- `/api/metrics/history` — Time-series data for UI charts

**Persistence:** SQLite database at `~/.moltis/metrics.db`, 7-day retention, 10-second intervals.

### 6.2 Tracing
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/docs/src/metrics-and-tracing.md` (Lines 303-341)

Tracing uses the standard Rust `tracing` crate. Span fields can be propagated to metric labels:

```rust
#[instrument(fields(operation = "fetch_user", component = "api"))]
async fn fetch_user(id: u64) -> User {
    counter!("api_calls_total").increment(1);
    // This metric will include operation="fetch_user", component="api"
}
```

---

## 7. LOGGING & DIAGNOSTICS

### 7.1 JSONL Audit Logging
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/gateway/src/server.rs` (Lines 3763, 4338-4341)

Command logger hook: "Logs all slash-command invocations to a JSONL audit file at `~/.moltis/logs/commands.log`"

Each entry records:
- timestamp
- command name
- arguments
- user ID
- result/error

### 7.2 Tracing Levels
Per CLAUDE.md:
- `error!` — unrecoverable
- `warn!` — unexpected but recoverable
- `info!` — operational milestones
- `debug!` — detailed diagnostics
- `trace!` — very verbose per-item data

**Common mistake:** Use `debug!` for expected "not configured" states (e.g., unconfigured provider), not `warn!`.

---

## 8. HEARTBEAT & SESSION KEEP-ALIVE

### 8.1 Heartbeat Configuration
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/config/src/template.rs` (Lines 490-510)

```toml
[heartbeat]
enabled = true
every = "30m"  # Interval between heartbeats
model = "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-20250514"  # Optional override
prompt = "..."  # Custom heartbeat prompt
ack_max_chars = 300  # Max chars for acknowledgment reply
deliver = false  # Deliver replies to a channel account
channel = "my-bot"  # Required when deliver = true
to = "123456789"  # Recipient ID
sandbox_enabled = true

[heartbeat.active_hours]
start = "08:00"  # Start time (HH:MM)
end = "24:00"  # End time
timezone = "local"  # "local" or IANA name
```

Heartbeats are **periodic health-check agent turns** to keep the agent "alive" and responsive. They run during configured active hours only.

---

## 9. SESSION COMPACTION & CONTEXT PRUNING

### 9.1 Context Window Error Detection
**File:** `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/butter-astronaut/crates/agents/src/runner.rs` (Line 141)

```rust
fn is_context_window_error(msg: &str) -> bool {
    // Detects context window exceeded errors from providers
}
```

When context window is exceeded (lines 802, 1477), the runner handles it — likely triggering session compaction or message summarization.

### 9.2 Message Storage as Neutral Format
Messages are stored as `serde_json::Value` (not typed `ChatMessage`) so they can be:
1. Persisted without schema coupling
2. Transformed (compacted, summarized, pruned) before re-use
3. Served to UI without re-serialization

No explicit `compact_session()` function was found in the code, but the architecture supports it:
- Hook `BeforeCompaction` and `AfterCompaction` are defined in template (lines 682, 688)
- Session messages are JSON-based, allowing transformations

---

## 10. KEY ARCHITECTURAL PATTERNS

### 10.1 No Per-Model Config Params
There are **NO per-model `params` or `extra_params` fields** in ProviderEntry. All configuration is at the provider level:

```rust
// NOT in schema:
[providers.anthropic.models.claude-opus]  // ❌ invalid
params = { reasoning_effort = "high" }

// INSTEAD, use per-agent presets:
[agents.presets.thinking]
model = "anthropic/claude-opus-4-5"
reasoning_effort = "high"  // ✅ valid, on AgentPreset
```

### 10.2 Config → Provider Instance Flow
```
ProviderEntry (config)
  ├── enabled, api_key, base_url, models, fetch_models
  ├── stream_transport, wire_api, tool_mode
  └── alias
    ↓
  LlmProvider impl (AnthropicProvider, OpenAiProvider, etc.)
    ├── Struct fields: api_key, model, base_url, client, alias
    ├── Builder methods: with_alias(), with_stream_transport(), with_tool_mode()
    └── Implements: complete(), stream(), supports_tools(), context_window()
```

**Order of instantiation:**
1. Check config `.enabled`
2. Resolve API key (config → env var → env_overrides)
3. Get base_url, models, stream_transport, tool_mode from config
4. For each model: create provider instance with those values
5. Register in ProviderRegistry with metadata

### 10.3 Secret Handling
- API keys wrapped in `secrecy::Secret<String>`
- `.expose_secret()` only at consumption point (HTTP request building)
- Manual `Debug` impl returns `[REDACTED]` to prevent accidental logging
- Serialization hides the secret

### 10.4 Metrics Label Strategy
Provider alias (if set) is used for metrics labels instead of provider name. This allows:
- Multiple instances of same provider (e.g., `anthropic-work`, `anthropic-personal`)
- Per-instance billing/quota tracking
- Separate Grafana dashboards

---

## 12. KEY TAKEAWAYS FOR DEVELOPERS

1. **No per-model params in config** — use agent presets for per-model overrides (reasoning_effort, system_prompt_suffix, etc.)
2. **Flat provider config** — all settings (enabled, api_key, base_url, models, stream_transport, tool_mode, alias) are on ProviderEntry
3. **API key resolution order:** config entry → env var → env_overrides
4. **Metrics are feature-gated** — zero overhead when disabled
5. **Provider aliases** enable multi-instance tracking without duplicate config sections
6. **Sessions stored as JSON** allowing transformations (compaction, pruning)
7. **Heartbeat** is a builtin periodic keep-alive mechanism with active hours support
8. **Tracing uses standard Rust patterns** with span field propagation to metrics
9. **Secrets** wrapped in `secrecy::Secret` with redaction at Debug/serialization
10. **No reasoning_effort on provider** — it's set per-agent preset, then passed to provider at call time

  1963→    fn register_builtin_providers(
  1964→        &mut self,
  1965→        config: &ProvidersConfig,
  1966→        env_overrides: &HashMap<String, String>,
  1967→        prefetched: &HashMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredModel>>,

  1970→        if config.is_enabled("anthropic")

  1973→        {
  1974→            let base_url = config
  1975→                .get("anthropic")
  1976→                .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())
  1977→                .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL"))
  1978→                .unwrap_or_else(|| "https://api.anthropic.com".into());
  1979→
  1980→            // Get alias if configured (for metrics differentiation).
  1981→            let alias = config.get("anthropic").and_then(|e| e.alias.clone());
  1982→            let provider_label = alias.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "anthropic".into());
  1983→            let preferred = configured_models_for_provider(config, "anthropic");
  1984→            let discovered = if should_fetch_models(config, "anthropic") {
  1985→                ANTHROPIC_MODELS
  1986→                    .iter()
  1987→                    .map(|(id, name)| DiscoveredModel::new(*id, *name))
  1988→                    .collect()
  1989→            } else {
  1990→                Vec::new()
  1991→            };
  1992→            let models = merge_preferred_and_discovered_models(preferred, discovered);
  1993→
  1994→            for model in models {
  1995→                let (model_id, display_name, created_at) =
  1996→                    (model.id, model.display_name, model.created_at);
  1997→                if self.has_provider_model(&provider_label, &model_id) {
  1998→                    continue;
  1999→                }
  2000→                let provider = Arc::new(anthropic::AnthropicProvider::with_alias(
  2001→                    key.clone(),
  2002→                    model_id.clone(),
  2003→                    base_url.clone(),
  2004→                    alias.clone(),
  2005→                ));
  2006→                self.register(
  2007→                    ModelInfo {
  2008→                        id: model_id,
  2009→                        provider: provider_label.clone(),
  2010→                        display_name,
  2011→                        created_at,
  2012→                    },
  2013→                    provider,
  2014→                );
  2015→            }
  2016→        }
  2017→
  2018→        // OpenAI — register all known OpenAI models when API key is available.
  2019→        if config.is_enabled("openai")
  2020→            && let Some(key) = resolve_api_key(config, "openai", "OPENAI_API_KEY", env_overrides)
  2021→        {
  2022→            let base_url = config
  2023→                .get("openai")
  2024→                .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())
  2025→                .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, "OPENAI_BASE_URL"))
  2026→                .unwrap_or_else(|| "https://api.openai.com/v1".into());
  2027→
  2028→            // Get alias if configured (for metrics differentiation).
  2029→            let alias = config.get("openai").and_then(|e| e.alias.clone());
  2030→            let provider_label = alias.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "openai".into());
  2031→            let stream_transport = config
  2032→                .get("openai")
  2033→                .map(|entry| entry.stream_transport)
  2034→                .unwrap_or(ProviderStreamTransport::Sse);
  2035→            let preferred = configured_models_for_provider(config, "openai");
  2036→            let discovered = if should_fetch_models(config, "openai") {
  2037→                // Use pre-fetched live models from parallel discovery.
  2038→                let fallback = openai::default_model_catalog();
  2039→                match prefetched.get("openai") {
  2040→                    Some(live) => {
  2041→                        let merged = merge_discovered_with_fallback_catalog(live.clone(), fallback);
  2042→                        tracing::debug!(model_count = merged.len(), "loaded openai models catalog");
  2043→                        merged
  2044→                    },
  2045→                    None => fallback,
  2046→                }
  2047→            } else {
  2048→                Vec::new()
  2049→            };
  2050→            let models = merge_preferred_and_discovered_models(preferred, discovered);
  2051→
  2052→            for model in models {

  2057→                }

  "new_string": "/// Prompt cache retention policy for providers that support client-controlled\n/// caching (Anthropic direct, Anthropic via OpenRouter/Bedrock).\n///\n/// - `none`: disable prompt caching (no `cache_control` breakpoints sent).\n/// - `short` (default for Anthropic): 5-minute ephemeral cache.\n/// - `long`: same as `short` today (Anthropic only supports ephemeral), but\n///   signals intent for longer retention when providers add TTL tiers.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n#[serde(rename_all = \"lowercase\")]\npub enum CacheRetention {\n    /// No prompt caching — skip `cache_control` breakpoints entirely.\n    None,\n    /// Short-lived ephemeral cache (5 min TTL on Anthropic). Default for Anthropic.\n    #[default]\n    Short,\n    /// Long-lived cache. Has no effect on\n    /// providers with automatic server-side caching (OpenAI, DeepSeek, Ollama).\n    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"is_default_cache_retention\")]\n    pub cache_retention: CacheRetention,\n}"

    23→impl AnthropicProvider {
    24→    pub fn new(api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>, model: String, base_url: String) -> Self {

    26→            api_key,
    27→            model,
    28→            base_url,

    31→            reasoning_effort: None,
    32→        }
    33→    }
    34→
    35→    /// Create a new provider with a custom alias for metrics.
    36→    pub fn with_alias(
    37→        api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
    38→        model: String,
    39→        base_url: String,
    40→        alias: Option<String>,
    41→    ) -> Self {
    42→        Self {
    43→            api_key,
    44→            model,
    45→            base_url,
    46→            client: crate::shared_http_client(),
    47→            alias,
    48→            reasoning_effort: None,
    49→        }
    50→    }
    51→
    52→    /// Apply `thinking` configuration to an Anthropic request body based on
    53→    /// the configured reasoning effort.
    54→    fn apply_thinking(&self, body: &mut serde_json::Value) {
    55→        use moltis_agents::model::ReasoningEffort;
    56→        let Some(effort) = self.reasoning_effort else {
    57→            return;
    58→        };
    59→        let budget_tokens: u64 = match effort {
    60→            ReasoningEffort::Low => 4096,
    61→            ReasoningEffort::Medium => 10240,
    62→            ReasoningEffort::High => 32768,
    63→        };
    64→        body["thinking"] = serde_json::json!({
    65→            "type": "enabled",
    66→            "budget_tokens": budget_tokens,
    67→        });
    68→        // Extended thinking requires higher max_tokens than budget_tokens.
    69→        if let Some(max_tokens) = body["max_tokens"].as_u64()
    70→            && max_tokens <= budget_tokens
    71→        {
    72→            body["max_tokens"] = serde_json::json!(budget_tokens + 4096);
    73→        }
    74→    }
    75→}

When `None`, caching is disabled.\n    cache_retention: moltis_config::CacheRetention,\n}\n\nimpl AnthropicProvider {\n    pub fn new(api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>, model: String, base_url: String) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            api_key,\n            model,\n            base_url,\n            client: crate::shared_http_client(),\n            alias: None,\n            reasoning_effort: None,\n            cache_retention: moltis_config::CacheRetention::Short,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create a new provider with a custom alias for metrics.\n    pub fn with_alias(\n        api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,\n        model: String,\n        base_url: String,\n        alias: Option<String>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            api_key,\n            model,\n            base_url,\n            client: crate::shared_http_client(),\n            alias,\n            reasoning_effort: None,\n            cache_retention: moltis_config::CacheRetention::Short,\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn with_cache_retention(mut self, cache_retention: moltis_config::CacheRetention) -> Self {\n        self.cache_retention = cache_retention;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Returns `true` when prompt caching is enabled (short or long retention).\n    fn caching_enabled(&self) -> bool {\n        !matches!(self.cache_retention, moltis_config::CacheRetention::None)\n    }"

   130→}
   131→
   132→/// Convert `ChatMessage` list to Anthropic format.
   133→///
   134→/// Returns `(system_blocks, anthropic_messages)`. System messages are extracted
   135→/// as a content-block array (Anthropic takes them as a top-level `system` field).
   136→/// The last system block gets `cache_control` for prompt caching. Tool messages
   137→/// become user messages with `tool_result` content blocks. Assistant messages with
   138→/// tool calls become `content` arrays with `tool_use` blocks.
   139→///
   140→/// The last user message also gets `cache_control` on its final content block,
   141→/// so the conversation prefix is cached across turns.
   142→fn to_anthropic_messages(
   143→    messages: &[ChatMessage],
   144→) -> (Option<Vec<serde_json::Value>>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {
   145→    let mut system_text: Option<String> = None;
   146→    let mut out = Vec::new();
   147→
   148→    for msg in messages {
   149→        match msg {
   150→            ChatMessage::System { content } => {
   151→                system_text = Some(match system_text {
   152→                    Some(existing) => format!("{existing}\n\n{content}"),
   153→                    None => content.clone(),
   154→                });
   155→            },
   156→            ChatMessage::User { content } => match content {
   157→                UserContent::Text(text) => {
   158→                    out.push(serde_json::json!({"role": "user", "content": text}));
   159→                },

   213→                out.push(serde_json::json!({
   214→                    "role": "user",
   215→                    "content": [{
   216→                        "type": "tool_result",
   217→                        "tool_use_id": tool_call_id,
   218→                        "content": content,
   219→                    }]
   220→                }));
   221→            },
   222→        }
   223→    }
   224→
   225→    // Build system as a content-block array with cache_control on the last block.
   226→    let system_blocks = system_text.map(|text| {
   227→        vec![serde_json::json!({
   228→            "type": "text",
   229→            "text": text,
   230→            "cache_control": { "type": "ephemeral" }
   231→        })]
   232→    });
   233→
   234→    // Add cache_control to the last user message's final content block.
   235→    inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message(&mut out);
   236→
   237→    (system_blocks, out)
   238→}
   239→
   240→/// Find the last user message in the output array and add `cache_control`
   241→/// to its final content block, enabling Anthropic prompt caching on the
   242→/// conversation prefix.
   243→fn inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message(messages: &mut [serde_json::Value]) {
   244→    let cache_control = serde_json::json!({ "type": "ephemeral" });
   245→
   246→    let Some(last_user) = messages
   247→        .iter_mut()
   248→        .rev()
   249→        .find(|m| m.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("user"))
   250→    else {
   251→        return;
   252→    };
   253→
   254→    match last_user.get_mut("content") {
   255→        // String content — convert to content-block array with cache_control.
   256→        Some(content) if content.is_string() => {
   257→            let text = content.as_str().unwrap_or_default().to_string();
   258→            last_user["content"] = serde_json::json!([{
   259→                "type": "text",
   260→                "text": text,
   261→                "cache_control": cache_control
   262→            }]);
   263→        },
   264→        // Array content — add cache_control to the last block.
   265→        Some(content) if content.is_array() => {
   266→            if let Some(last_block) = content.as_array_mut().and_then(|arr| arr.last_mut()) {
   267→                last_block["cache_control"] = cache_control;
   268→            }
   269→        },
   270→        _ => {},
   271→    }
   272→}
   273→
   274→#[async_trait]
   275→impl LlmProvider for AnthropicProvider {
   276→    fn name(&self) -> &str {
   277→        self.alias.as_deref().unwrap_or("anthropic")
   278→    }
   279→

System messages are\n/// extracted (Anthropic takes them as a top-level `system` field).\n///\n/// When `caching` is true, the system prompt is returned as a content-block\n/// array with `cache_control` on the last block, and the last user message\n/// gets `cache_control` on its final content block (enabling prompt caching\n/// on the conversation prefix). When `caching` is false, system is returned\n/// as a plain string and no `cache_control` blocks are injected.\nfn to_anthropic_messages(\n    messages: &[ChatMessage],\n    caching: bool,\n) -> (Option<serde_json::Value>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {\n    let mut system_text: Option<String> = None;\n    let mut out = Vec::new();\n\n    for msg in messages {\n        match msg {\n            ChatMessage::System { content } => {\n                system_text = Some(match system_text {\n                    Some(existing) => format!(\"{existing}\\n\\n{content}\"),\n                    None => content.clone(),\n                });\n            },\n            ChatMessage::User { content } => match content {\n                UserContent::Text(text) => {\n                    out.push(serde_json::json!({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": text}));\n                },\n                UserContent::Multimodal(parts) => {\n                    let blocks: Vec<serde_json::Value> = parts\n                        .iter()\n                        .map(|part| match part {\n                            ContentPart::Text(text) => {\n                                serde_json::json!({\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": text})\n                            },\n                            ContentPart::Image { media_type, data } => {\n                                serde_json::json!({\n                                    \"type\": \"image\",\n                                    \"source\": {\n                                        \"type\": \"base64\",\n                                        \"media_type\": media_type,\n                                        \"data\": data,\n                                    }\n                                })\n                            },\n                        })\n                        .collect();\n                    out.push(serde_json::json!({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": blocks}));\n                },\n            },\n            ChatMessage::Assistant {\n                content,\n                tool_calls,\n            } => {\n                if tool_calls.is_empty() {\n                    out.push(serde_json::json!({\n                        \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                        \"content\": content.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"),\n                    }));\n                } else {\n                    let mut blocks = Vec::new();\n                    if let Some(text) = content\n                        && !text.is_empty()\n                    {\n                        blocks.push(serde_json::json!({\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": text}));\n                    }\n                    for tc in tool_calls {\n                        blocks.push(serde_json::json!({\n                            \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n                            \"id\": tc.id,\n                            \"name\": tc.name,\n                            \"input\": tc.arguments,\n                        }));\n                    }\n                    out.push(serde_json::json!({\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": blocks}));\n                }\n            },\n            ChatMessage::Tool {\n                tool_call_id,\n                content,\n            } => {\n                out.push(serde_json::json!({\n                    \"role\": \"user\",\n                    \"content\": [{\n                        \"type\": \"tool_result\",\n                        \"tool_use_id\": tool_call_id,\n                        \"content\": content,\n                    }]\n                }));\n            },\n        }\n    }\n\n    let system_value = system_text.map(|text| {\n        if caching {\n            // Content-block array with cache_control on the last block.\n            serde_json::json!([{\n                \"type\": \"text\",\n                \"text\": text,\n                \"cache_control\": { \"type\": \"ephemeral\" }\n            }])\n        } else {\n            // Plain string — no caching.\n            serde_json::Value::String(text)\n        }\n    });\n\n    if caching {\n        inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message(&mut out);\n    }\n\n    (system_value, out)\n}"
}

   248→/// to its final content block, enabling Anthropic prompt caching on the

   250→fn inject_cache_control_on_last_user_message(messages: &mut [serde_json::Value]) {

   252→
   253→    let Some(last_user) = messages
   254→        .iter_mut()
   255→        .rev()

   257→    else {
   258→        return;
   259→    };
   260→
   261→    match last_user.get_mut("content") {
   262→        // String content — convert to content-block array with cache_control.
   263→        Some(content) if content.is_string() => {
   264→            let text = content.as_str().unwrap_or_default().to_string();
   265→            last_user["content"] = serde_json::json!([{
   266→                "type": "text",
   267→                "text": text,
   268→                "cache_control": cache_control
   269→            }]);
   270→        },
   271→        // Array content — add cache_control to the last block.
   272→        Some(content) if content.is_array() => {
   273→            if let Some(last_block) = content.as_array_mut().and_then(|arr| arr.last_mut()) {
   274→                last_block["cache_control"] = cache_control;

Here's a summary of everything implemented:

## Changes Summary

### 1. `CacheRetention` config enum (`none`/`short`/`long`)

**`crates/config/src/schema.rs`:**
- New `CacheRetention` enum with `None`, `Short` (default), `Long` variants
- Added `cache_retention` field to `ProviderEntry` with serde skip-if-default
- Updated `Debug` impl and `Default` impl
- Helper `is_default_cache_retention()` for serde

**`crates/config/src/validate.rs`:**
- Added `cache_retention` to `build_schema_map()` provider entry
- 2 new tests: field acceptance + all values parse correctly

**`crates/config/src/template.rs`:**
- Added `cache_retention = "short"` comment to Anthropic provider template

**`crates/config/src/lib.rs`:**
- Exported `CacheRetention` from the crate

### 2. Anthropic provider respects `cache_retention`

**`crates/providers/src/anthropic.rs`:**
- Added `cache_retention` field to `AnthropicProvider`
- `with_cache_retention()` builder method
- `caching_enabled()` helper
- `to_anthropic_messages()` now takes `caching: bool` — when `false`, system is plain string and no `cache_control` blocks are injected
- Cache diagnostics: `debug!` log with `cache_read`/`cache_write` token counts after each completion
- 3 new tests: `caching_disabled_returns_plain_string_system`, `cache_retention_none_skips_cache_control`, `cache_retention_short_enables_caching`

### 3. OpenRouter respects `cache_retention`

**`crates/providers/src/openai.rs`:**
- Added `cache_retention` field to `OpenAiProvider`
- `with_cache_retention()` builder method
- `apply_openrouter_cache_control()` now skips when `cache_retention == None`

### 4. Provider registration passes `cache_retention` from config

**`crates/providers/src/lib.rs`:**
- Anthropic registration reads `cache_retention` from config, passes to provider
- OpenAI-compat registration (OpenRouter, etc.) reads and passes `cache_retention`

### What matches OpenClaw now

| Feature | Status |
|---|---|
| `cacheRetention` config (`none`/`short`/`long`) | Done |
| Default `short` for Anthropic | Done |
| `none` disables cache_control injection | Done |
| System prompt `cache_control` breakpoint | Done |
| Last user message `cache_control` breakpoint | Done |
| OpenRouter Anthropic passthrough | Done |
| Non-Anthropic Bedrock forced to `none` | N/A (no Bedrock provider) |
| Cache token tracking in Usage | Already existed |
| Cache diagnostics (debug logging) | Done |
| Heartbeat keep-warm | Already existed (general-purpose) |
| Context pruning (`cache-ttl` mode) | Separate feature — out of scope for prompt caching |

  "description": "Create commit"
}
